Abstract: While multi-modal 3D semantic occupancy prediction typically enhances robustness by fusing camera and LiDAR inputs, its effectiveness is fundamentally constrained by environmental variability. Specifically, camera sensors suffer from severe low-light degradation, while LiDAR sensors encounter significant backscatter noise during heavy precipitation. These adverse conditions create a modality trust problem, as static fusion strategies fail to adaptively re-weight inputs when a specific sensor becomes unreliable. To address this, we propose a VLM-assisted framework leveraging the pre-trained CLIP latent space to guide multi-sensor integration via linguistic environmental cues. We utilize a parameter-efficient adapter to align weather-specific text embeddings with sensor features, coupled with a gating strategy that decomposes environmental uncertainty into two factors: visibility and illumination. This enables the model to dynamically modulate the fusion ratio - prioritizing semantic camera features in clear daylight and shifting to geometric LiDAR priors during rainy nights. Evaluations on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate the versatility of our approach, as implementing our proposed framework on the OccMamba and M-CONet architectures achieves mIoU scores of 26.3 and 21.1, respectively, significantly outperforming their traditional baselines.

</details>


### [111] [STABLE: Simulation-Ready Tabletop Layout Generation via a Semantics-Physics Dual System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16137)
*Zhen Luo,Yixuan Yang,Xudong Xu,Jinkun Hao,Zhaoyang Lyu,Feng Zheng,Jiangmiao Pang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STABLE提出了一种语义-物理双系统方法，用于从任务指令生成仿真就绪的桌面场景，通过语义推理器（微调LLM）生成粗略布局，再经物理校正器（基于流的去噪模型）优化姿态，交替迭代逐步扩展场景，显著提升了物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务到场景生成方法仅依赖LLM预测场景布局，因LLM在3D空间推理上的固有局限，不可避免地产生物体碰撞或漂浮问题。

Method: 提出STABLE双系统框架：(i) 语义推理器——在结构化桌面场景数据集上微调的LLM，从任务指令生成粗略布局；(ii) 物理校正器——物理感知的基于流的去噪模型，输出姿态更新以优化布局，确保物理合理性同时保持语义对齐。采用渐进式生成范式，交替运行两个模块，从任务关键物体逐步扩展到背景物体。

Result: 实验表明STABLE成功生成严格符合任务指令的仿真就绪桌面场景，并在物理有效性上显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: STABLE通过语义与物理双系统交替协作的渐进生成策略，有效解决了LLM在3D空间推理上的不足，为从自然语言指令生成物理合理的仿真场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generating simulation-ready tabletop scenes from task instructions is an intriguing and promising research direction in the field of Embodied AI. However, existing task-to-scene generation methods rely exclusively on large language models (LLMs) to predict scene layouts, inevitably yielding object collisions or floating due to LLMs' inherent limitations in 3D spatial reasoning. In this paper, we present STABLE, a semantics-physics dual-system tailored for simulation-ready tabletop scene generation. STABLE consists of two complementary modules: (i) a Semantic Reasoner, a fine-tuned LLM trained on a structured tabletop scene dataset to generate coarse layouts from input task instructions, and (ii) a Physics Corrector, a physics-aware flow-based denoising model that outputs pose updates to refine layouts, which ensures the physical plausibility of scenes while preserves semantic alignment with task instructions. STABLE adopts a progressive generation paradigm: by alternating between the Semantic Reasoner and Physics Corrector, it incrementally expands the scene from task-critical objects to background objects. Experiments demonstrate that STABLE successfully generates simulation-ready tabletop scenes that strictly conform to task instructions and significantly enhances the physical validity of scenes over prior art.

</details>


### [112] [Registers Matter for Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16147)
*Nikita Starodubcev,Ilia Sudakov,Ilya Drobyshevskiy,Artem Babenko,Dmitry Baranchuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散变换器（DiTs）中寄存器令牌（register tokens）的作用。与视觉变换器（ViTs）不同，DiTs不会出现patch-token异常值，但寄存器令牌仍能显著提升像素空间DiTs的收敛速度和生成质量。通过分析发现，寄存器令牌在高噪声水平下能产生更干净的特征图，且近期像素空间DiT架构已隐含了类似寄存器的机制。作者据此提出了一种参数高效的双流架构，专门处理寄存器令牌，以极小的运行时开销提升了像素空间生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉变换器（ViTs）中存在高范数patch-token异常值问题，寄存器令牌可有效缓解。随着扩散模型采用变换器架构并向像素空间训练发展，其形式越来越接近ViTs，因此探究寄存器令牌是否对扩散变换器（DiTs）同样有效。

Method: 首先对比分析DiTs与ViTs在patch-token异常值方面的差异；其次在像素空间DiTs中引入寄存器令牌并评估其对收敛和生成质量的影响；然后通过分析中间表示探究寄存器令牌的作用机制；最后提出一种参数高效的双流架构，将寄存器令牌的处理进行专门化。

Result: DiTs不表现出patch-token异常值；寄存器令牌显著提升像素空间DiTs的收敛速度和生成质量；寄存器令牌在高噪声水平下产生更干净的特征图；近期像素空间DiT架构已隐含类似寄存器的机制；所提双流架构以极小运行时开销提升生成质量。

Conclusion: 寄存器令牌在DiTs中有效的原因不同于ViTs（并非解决异常值问题），而是通过在高噪声水平下提供更干净的特征图来提升生成质量。同时，现有先进架构已部分吸收该机制，但所提双流架构仍可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are known to exhibit high-norm patch-token outliers that degrade feature map quality, a problem effectively mitigated by \textit{register tokens}. As diffusion models increasingly adopt transformer architectures and move toward pixel-space training, they become closer in form to ViTs, raising the question of whether register tokens are also useful for Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). In this work, we show that DiTs differ from ViTs in a key respect: they do not exhibit patch-token outliers. Interestingly, register tokens significantly improve convergence and generation quality of pixel-space DiTs. By analyzing intermediate representations, we find that register tokens produce cleaner feature maps at high noise levels, which may contribute to their effectiveness in pixel-space generation. We further observe that recent pixel-space DiT architectures implicitly incorporate register-like mechanisms, which may partially account for their strong empirical performance. Motivated by these insights, we investigate a parameter-efficient dual-stream architecture that specializes processing for register tokens and improves pixel-space generation quality with negligible runtime overhead.

</details>


### [113] [Res$^2$CLIP: Few-Shot Generalist Anomaly Detection with Residual-to-Residual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16171)
*Xinyue Liu,Jianyuan Wang,Biao Leng,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Res²CLIP，一种将多模态对齐完全转移到统一残差空间的无监督异常检测框架，通过残差到残差的对齐机制同时解决跨粒度不匹配和跨类别泛化退化问题，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based少样本通用异常检测方法面临两大挑战：1)粗粒度的统一文本提示难以适应细粒度的前景-背景差异，导致跨粒度不匹配；2)在辅助数据集上微调会因领域偏移破坏CLIP固有的开放世界泛化能力，导致跨类别泛化退化。

Method: 提出残差到残差对齐框架Res²CLIP，包含三个分支：文本提示分支、视觉提示分支和残差到残差对齐分支。所有可学习优化被约束在残差域内，通过残差对齐优化目标迫使模型关注相对异常偏差而非优化类别特定特征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明该架构有效。

Conclusion: 将多模态对齐完全转移到统一残差空间，残差表示能自然消除跨区域的细粒度正常特征差异和类别特定偏差，同步解决跨粒度不匹配和跨类别泛化退化问题。

Abstract: Few-shot Generalist Anomaly Detection requires models to generalize to novel categories without retraining, posing significant challenges in real-world scenarios with scarce samples and rapidly changing categories. Existing CLIP-based methods face two major challenges: coarse-grained unified text prompts struggle to adapt to fine-grained foreground-background differences, causing cross-granularity mismatch; and fine-tuning on auxiliary datasets disrupts CLIP's inherent open-world generalization due to domain shift, leading to cross-category generalization degradation. To address these, we propose to shift multimodal alignment entirely into a unified residual space, where residual representations naturally eliminate fine-grained normal feature differences across regions and class-specific biases, simultaneously resolving both problems. Based on this insight, Res$^2$CLIP, the first residual-to-residual alignment framework that symmetrically bridges visual and text modalities within CLIP's residual space, is designed. The framework is developed from a residual perspective into three branches: a text prompt-based branch, a visual prompt-based branch, and a novel residual-to-residual alignment branch. All learnable optimizations are constrained within the residual domain, and the residual alignment optimization objectives are designed to force the model to focus on relative anomaly deviations rather than optimizing class-specific features. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our architecture. The code is available at https://github.com/hito2448/Res2CLIP.

</details>


### [114] [MAgSeg: Segmentation of Agricultural Landscapes in High-Resolution Satellite Imagery using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16179)
*Piyush Tiwary,Utkarsh Ahuja,Depanshu Sani,Aishwarya Jayagopal,Sagar Gubbi,Subhashini Venugopalan,Alok Talekar,Vaibhav Rajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAgSeg是一种无需辅助视觉解码器的新型MLLM分割方法，通过高效架构和指令微调数据格式，解决Global South农业景观分割中地块碎片化、类内方差高和标注数据稀缺等问题，在三个国家数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Global South的农业景观分割面临三个核心挑战：地块碎片化、高类内方差和标注训练数据稀缺。现有MLLM方法存在上下文长度瓶颈和卫星特征领域对齐差距，无法有效处理高分辨率遥感影像中的复杂小农农业场景。

Method: MAgSeg提出一种无解码器的MLLM分割架构，无需辅助视觉解码器即可直接分割高分辨率卫星影像。同时设计了一种新型指令微调数据格式，使模型能从图像的全局上下文中学习，同时仅为图像内的一个patch生成文本token，从而实现高分辨率影像上的可扩展微调。

Result: 在涵盖三个Global South国家的数据集上，MAgSeg显著优于现有最先进的MLLM基线方法，验证了其在复杂小农农业环境中的可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: MAgSeg通过架构创新和数据格式设计，有效解决了MLLM在农业景观分割中的上下文长度瓶颈和领域对齐问题，为Global South的小农农业环境映射提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Agricultural landscape segmentation in the Global South is challenging as it is characterized by fragmented plots, high intra-class variance, and a scarcity of labeled training data. Recent advances in segmentation have been made by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, current approaches encounter critical context length bottlenecks and a domain alignment gap in understanding satellite features. We address these limitations through MAgSeg, a novel, decoder-free MLLM segmentation approach. MAgSeg is an architecturally efficient approach that enables standard MLLMs to perform segmentation of complex smallholder agricultural landscapes from high-resolution satellite imagery, without requiring auxiliary vision decoders. We introduce a novel instruction tuning data format designed to enable scalable fine-tuning and post-training on high resolution satellite imagery, which enables MAgSeg to learn from the global context of the image while generating text tokens for only a patch within the image. Extensive evaluations on datasets spanning three countries in the Global South demonstrate that MAgSeg significantly outperforms state-of-the-art MLLM baselines, offering a scalable solution to map smallholder agricultural environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Autonomous Intelligent Agents for Natural-Language-Driven Web Execution with Integrated Security Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15281)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种AI驱动的自主测试框架，通过五种集成策略和容器化worker架构，将脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，并将自然语言安全测试扩展到OWASP Top 10漏洞检测，实现85%的认证绕过和95%的输入验证缺陷检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web测试套件因UI重构、时序变化等问题迅速腐化，导致开发团队在数周内放弃整个测试套件，亟需一种能够自动适应变化、减少人工维护的测试框架。

Method: 采用五种集成策略：导航可靠性、上下文感知选择器生成、生成后验证、智能等待注入和失败学习；基于容器化worker架构实现编排与浏览器执行解耦；支持自然语言描述的安全测试场景自动转换为OWASP Top 10浏览器探测。

Result: 在4个生产应用和176个场景下，脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，导航失败减少8倍，时序竞争条件消除80%，测试创建时间减少75%；安全测试中认证绕过检出率85%，输入验证缺陷检出率95%，假阳性率低于12%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了测试套件腐化问题，显著提升了测试生成效率和稳定性，且自然语言驱动的安全测试是该领域的创新贡献。

Abstract: Modern web test suites rot. A UI refactor breaks locators, a timing change causes race conditions, and within weeks developers abandon the suite entirely. This paper presents an AI-driven autonomous testing framework that addresses these failure modes through five integrated strategies - navigation reliability, context-aware selector generation, post-generation validation, smart wait injection, and failure learning - implemented over a containerised worker architecture that decouples orchestration from long-running browser execution. Evaluated across four production applications and 176 scenarios, the framework improves script generation success from 55% to 93%, achieves an 8x reduction in navigation failures, eliminates 80% of timing-related race conditions, and reduces test creation time by 75% compared to manual Selenium authoring. The framework extends naturally to security validation: testers describe attack scenarios in plain English - "try accessing another user's invoice" - which the agent converts to OWASP Top 10-aligned browser probes, detecting 85% of authentication bypass vulnerabilities and 95% of input validation flaws with false positive rates below 12%. Natural-language-driven security testing of this kind represents, to our knowledge, a novel contribution to the field.

</details>


### [116] [Distance-Preserving Digests: A Primitive for BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15329)
*Ryan Patrick Mercier*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代碰撞抵抗哈希的"距离保持交易摘要"原语，基于8维空间中的交换向量和，具有距离与分歧成正比、加权平均精确、集合差异可通过布隆过滤器差分识别的特性。基于此设计了三个应用：单轮最终性BFT协议Proxima、仅需10个验证者的树状共识、以及每对分片128字节的跨分片一致性验证。在N=100,000时，Proxima Tree消息量比HotStuff少2.2倍，单核最终性0.9秒对比18秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞抵抗哈希破坏了验证者状态之间的距离信息：即使两个验证者在20个交易中19个一致，产生的哈希也是不相关的，无法区分于完全没有共享内容的验证者。这导致了BFT文献中三个设计约束：验证者必须在投票前同步状态、协议完成前无法衡量协议质量、分层委员会必须足够大以支持独立BFT从而限制了树深度。本文旨在通过设计一种新的距离保持摘要原语来消除这些约束。

Method: 用8维空间中的交换向量和替代碰撞抵抗哈希，设计了距离保持交易摘要原语，其具备三个核心特性：距离与不一致程度成正比、支持精确加权平均、可以通过布隆过滤器差分识别集合差异。基于此构建了三个应用：Proxima（两阶段BFT协议，同意时单轮最终性）、树状共识（每组10个验证者，通过距离过滤替代每组的独立BFT）、跨分片一致性验证（每对分片仅128字节）。

Result: 安全性证明：少于N/3的拜占庭验证者无法导致冲突的最终确认，该性质独立于Phase 1聚类或树拓扑。在N=100,000规模下，Proxima Tree消息量比HotStuff少2.2倍（结构性优势，不受并行化影响）；单核最终性0.9秒对比HotStuff的18秒；多核BLS方案缩小但未消除差距。

Conclusion: 距离保持交易摘要作为一种新原语，成功替代了碰撞抵抗哈希在BFT共识中的应用，消除了传统BFT的三个设计约束，并在大规模场景下显著降低了消息复杂度和最终性延迟。

Abstract: Every BFT consensus protocol uses collision-resistant hashes to compare validator state. Collision resistance destroys distance: two validators agreeing on 19 of 20 transactions produce unrelated hashes, indistinguishable from validators sharing nothing. This forces three design constraints across the BFT literature: validators must synchronize state before voting, agreement quality cannot be measured until votes are counted, and hierarchical committees must be large enough for independent BFT, limiting tree depth. This paper introduces distance-preserving transaction digests, a primitive that replaces collision-resistant hashes with commutative vector sums in 8-dimensional space. The primitive has three properties hashes lack: distance is proportional to disagreement, weighted means are exact, and set differences are identifiable via bloom filter diff. We demonstrate three applications: a two-phase BFT protocol (Proxima) that achieves single-round finality when validators agree; tree-structured consensus with groups of 10 validators (vs 128 in Ethereum), enabled because distance filtering replaces per-group BFT; and cross-shard consistency verification at 128 bytes per shard pair, replacing the per-transaction coordination of two-phase commit. Safety is proved: fewer than N/3 Byzantine validators cannot cause conflicting finalization, independent of Phase 1 clustering or tree topology. At N =100,000, Proxima Tree uses 2.2x fewer messages than HotStuff (a structural property unaffected by parallelism). Single-core finality is 0.9s vs 18s for HotStuff; multi-core BLS narrows but does not eliminate this gap.

</details>


### [117] [Hidden in Memory: Sleeper Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15338)
*Sidharth Pulipaka,Stanislau Hlebik,Leonidas Raghav,Sahar Abdelnabi,Vyas Raina,Ivaxi Sheth,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出并研究了"休眠记忆中毒"攻击：通过操纵外部内容让LLM助手存储虚假用户记忆，这种攻击可潜伏并在后续多次对话中被触发。实验表明，GPT-5.5上记忆注入成功率达99.8%，Kimi-K2.6达95%，成功检索后导致恶意行为的概率为60-89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地配备持久记忆功能以支持个性化和连续性，但这种状态性引入了新的安全风险——对抗性内容可能破坏助手的记忆并影响未来交互。现有研究缺乏对这种延迟性攻击的系统性评估。

Method: 提出"休眠记忆中毒"攻击范式，操纵外部上下文（文档、网页、仓库等）使助手存储关于用户的虚构记忆。攻击分为三个阶段：记忆写入（验证中毒记忆是否被存储）、记忆检索（验证后续对话中是否被召回）、记忆利用（验证被召回的记忆是否导致攻击者预期的智能体行为）。

Result: GPT-5.5上记忆注入成功率达99.8%，Kimi-K2.6上达95%。在成功检索的场景中，中毒记忆导致攻击者预期的恶意行为的比例为60-89%。

Conclusion: 持久记忆可作为跨多个未来对话的长期攻击面，休眠记忆中毒攻击相比传统提示注入更具隐蔽性和持续性，需要新的防御机制来保障状态化LLM助手的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly augmented with persistent memory, allowing assistants to store user-specific information across sessions for personalization and continuity. This statefulness introduces a new security risk: adversarial content can corrupt what an assistant remembers and thereby influence future interactions. We propose and study sleeper memory poisoning, a delayed attack in which an adversary manipulates external context, such as a document, webpage, or repository, to cause the assistant to store a fabricated memory about the user. Unlike conventional prompt injection, the attack can remain dormant and re-emerge across multiple later conversations. We evaluate the full attack pipeline: whether poisoned memories are written, later retrieved, and ultimately used to steer the following conversations. Across stateful LLM assistants, poisoned memories were added up to 99.8% on GPT-5.5 and 95% on Kimi-K2.6. Crucially, among successful retrievals, poisoned memories cause attacker-intended agentic actions in 60-89% of evaluations across models. These results show that persistent memory can act as a long-term attack surface across multiple future conversations.

</details>


### [118] [uGen: An Agentic Framework for Generating Microarchitectural Attack PoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15503)
*Debopriya Roy Dipta,Thore Tiemann,Eduard Marin,Thomas Eisenbarth,Berk Gulmezoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出uGen，首个基于LLM驱动的微架构攻击代码自动生成框架。通过检索增强的多智能体设计弥补LLM在攻击原语上的知识缺口，在缓存攻击和推测执行攻击上实现最高100%的成功率（Spectre-v1），且生成一个PoC成本仅需1.25美元、不到4分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构攻击代码开发劳动密集、需深厚专业知识且环境敏感，现有攻击缺乏可移植性，难以进行系统性漏洞评估。LLM虽有潜力降低门槛，但能否可靠生成功能正确的微架构攻击代码尚不清楚。

Method: 首先系统研究GPT、Claude、Qwen3等SOTA模型，发现LLM在攻击原语上频繁错误生成或错误定位。基于此分析，uGen采用检索增强的多智能体设计，注入缺失的领域知识，合成功能正确的微架构攻击PoC代码。

Result: 在多种微架构、脆弱函数和LLM平台上评估，uGen在Spectre-v1（Claude Sonnet-4）上达到100%成功率，Prime+Probe（Qwen3-Coder）上达到80%成功率，生成成功PoC代码成本1.25美元、耗时不到4分钟。

Conclusion: uGen证明了LLM结合检索增强与多智能体框架能够高效自动生成功能正确的微架构攻击代码，为系统性漏洞评估提供了可扩展的低成本方案。

Abstract: Microarchitectural attacks continue to evolve, uncovering new exploitation vectors in modern processors. From a defensive perspective, assessing a system's susceptibility to such attacks remains challenging. Developing functional attack implementations is labor-intensive, requires deep microarchitectural expertise, and is highly sensitive to execution environments. Consequently, existing attacks often lack portability, limiting systematic and scalable vulnerability assessment. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) suggest a potential avenue for lowering these barriers. However, it remains unclear whether LLMs can reliably generate functionally correct microarchitectural attack code suitable for rigorous vulnerability testing.
  In this work, we present uGen, the first LLM-driven framework for automated microarchitectural attack code generation. A key challenge we address is identifying attack-specific knowledge gaps in LLMs. Through a systematic study of state-of-the-art models (GPT, Claude, and Qwen3), we find that LLMs frequently misgenerate or misplace critical attack primitives. Guided by this analysis, uGen employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent design that injects missing domain knowledge to synthesize functionally correct microarchitectural attack PoCs tailored to defender requirements. We evaluate uGen on cache-based and speculative-execution attacks across diverse set of microarchitectures, vulnerable functions, and LLM platforms. In the deployment stage, uGen achieves up to 100% success rate for Spectre-v1 (Claude Sonnet-4) and 80% for Prime+Probe (Qwen3-Coder). Finally, we demonstrate that uGen can generate a successful PoC code with a cost of $1.25 in under four minutes.

</details>


### [119] [Compositional Jailbreaking: An Empirical Analysis of Mutator Chain Interactions in Aligned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15598)
*Reinelle Jan Bugnot,Soohyeon Choi,Hoon Wei Lim,Yue Duan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对大语言模型的越狱攻击中，弱攻击变换的链式组合（mutator chaining）如何相互作用，发现大多数组合会产生破坏性干扰或结构不兼容，但少数组合能产生协同效应提升攻击成功率，揭示了安全对齐的结构特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已探索手工和自动越狱策略，但简单攻击之间的组合交互潜力尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究弱越狱变换按序组合时的相互作用模式。

Method: 实现12个基线越狱变换器（mutator），在有害提示基准上评估所有有序对组合，针对三个流行大语言模型进行测试；引入完整性和有效性指标，分别捕获变换持久性和攻击有效性。

Result: 变换交互模式高度不均匀：大多数组合无法优于单一变换，表现出破坏性干扰或结构不兼容；少数组合产生协同效应，提升攻击成功率；常见的失败模式揭示了单策略评估中不可见的安全对齐结构特性。

Conclusion: 揭示了对抗性提示组合的细微动态，为构建更鲁棒的安全防御提供了新见解。

Abstract: Jailbreaking attacks on large language models pose a significant threat to AI safety by enabling the generation of harmful or restricted content. While prior work has explored both handcrafted and automated jailbreak strategies, the potential for compositional interaction between simple attacks remains underexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of mutator chaining, in which weak jailbreak transformations are applied sequentially to characterize how they interact: whether they reinforce one another, interfere destructively, or produce no meaningful change. We implement twelve baseline mutators and evaluate all ordered pairs on a benchmark of harmful prompts against three popular LLM models. Our framework introduces metrics for completeness and validity that capture both transformation persistence and attack effectiveness. Results reveal that the interaction landscape is highly non-uniform, while most combinations fail to outperform individual mutators, exhibiting destructive interference or structural incompatibility, a small fraction produce synergistic effects that improve attack success rates. Equally important, the prevalent failure modes reveal structural properties of safety alignment that are not apparent from single-strategy evaluations. These findings highlight the nuanced dynamics of adversarial prompt composition and offer new insights for building more robust safety defenses.

</details>


### [120] [Rethinking the Security of DP-SGD: A Corrected Analysis of Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15648)
*Wenhao Wang,Shujie Cui,Hui Cui,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现DP-SGD的常见实现（如Meta的Opacus）在隐私分析时使用了子采样高斯机制（SGM）建模，但实际实现中包含了额外的归一化步骤（除以期望批次大小或实际批次大小），导致真实隐私泄露可能超过基于SGM的理论保证。作者重新分析了期望平均SGM（EASGM）和批次平均SGM（ASGM）的隐私保证，并通过实验验证了四款主流实现（包括Opacus v0.9.0至v1.5.4）存在超出SGM保证的经验性泄露，最终为最新实现推导了修正后的隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP-SGD的隐私分析通常假设其符合子采样高斯机制（SGM），但实际实现中使用了不同的归一化操作，导致理论分析与实践实现之间存在不匹配，可能造成隐私保证被高估。

Method: 1) 理论分析：分别对EASGM（除以期望批次大小）和ASGM（除以实际批次大小）重新推导隐私保证；2) 实验审计：对四款DP-SGD实现（包括Opacus v0.9.0至v1.5.4）进行经验性隐私泄露测量，验证理论结果。

Result: 1) 理论结果表明EASGM和ASGM的隐私保证在某些情况下弱于标准SGM；2) 实验发现四款主流实现的真实隐私泄露均超出SGM的理论保证；3) 为最新版Opacus推导了修正的隐私保证。

Conclusion: DP-SGD的实际实现可能比理论分析的隐私保证更弱，需要对实现细节（如归一化方式）进行更精确的建模，以确保隐私声明的可信性。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) is widely used to protect training data in machine learning. Its privacy guarantee is commonly analyzed through a security game in which an adversary infers whether a target record is included in the training dataset from the mechanism output. The resulting privacy leakage is characterized by a privacy curve, which reports the false negative rate as a function of the false positive rate.
  We identify a mismatch between this formal analysis and common DP-SGD implementations. Existing analyses often model DP-SGD and its variants as the Subsampled Gaussian Mechanism (SGM), where Gaussian noise is added to the sum of clipped gradients computed from a Poisson-sampled batch. In practice, however, many implementations apply an additional normalization step: the noisy gradient sum is divided either by the expected batch size or by the sampled batch size. These mechanisms are therefore better formalized as the Expected-Averaged SGM (EASGM) or the Batch-Averaged SGM (ASGM), respectively.
  We re-analyze the privacy guarantees of DP-SGD under the EASGM and ASGM formulations. Our theoretical results show that these guarantees can be weaker than the standard SGM-based guarantee, implying that the true privacy leakage may exceed the reported guarantee in some regimes. We further audit four state-of-the-art DP-SGD implementations, including Meta's Opacus library, and observe empirical leakage beyond the SGM-based guarantees. Finally, we audit Opacus versions v0.9.0 to v1.5.4 and derive a corrected privacy guarantee for the latest implementation.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Controlled Noise: Achieving Symmetric FHE through Dynamic Position Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15774)
*Mostefa Kara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于明文分片和动态插位机制的对称全同态加密方案，通过双调节器系统（指数调节器和系数调节器）解决乘法过程中的指数级噪声增长问题，并通过绑定机制确保安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全同态加密方案存在计算开销过大和噪声管理复杂的问题，亟需一种更高效且噪声可控的对称FHE方案。

Method: 基于模块化加密基础 c = mk + rp，引入明文分片与动态插位框架，将明文划分为多个片段置于不同逻辑位置；设计双调节器系统——指数调节器(t_i)将片段乘积重定向至新目标位置以防止密钥指数累积，系数调节器(d_i)对结果标量进行归一化；通过指数与系数的相互依赖的绑定机制保护密钥k免受代数和替换攻击。

Result: 该方案实现了乘法过程中噪声的指数级增长到可控的转变，同时通过绑定机制确保了安全性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种噪声可控、计算高效的对称FHE方案，通过分片和双调节器机制解决了传统FHE的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Traditional Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes often suffer from prohibitive computational overhead and complex noise management. In this paper, we propose a novel symmetric FHE through a mechanism of plaintext fragmentation and dynamic interposition. Our approach is built upon a modular encryption foundation, c = mk + rp, which is naturally additive but typically limited by exponential noise growth during multiplication. To resolve this, we introduce an interposition framework where the plaintext is partitioned into multiple fragments across distinct logical positions. We introduce a dual-regulator system to govern the multiplication process; exponent regulators (t_i) redirect the product of fragments to a new target position, preventing the accumulation of secret key exponents, while coefficient regulators (d_i) normalize the resulting scalars. Security is established through a binding mechanism where exponents and coefficients are mutually dependent, shielding the secret key k from algebraic manipulation and substitution attacks.

</details>


### [122] [A Cross-Modal Prompt Injection Attack against Large Vision-Language Models with Image-Only Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16090)
*Hao Yang,Zhuo Ma,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Guancheng Wang,JianFeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CrossMPI的跨模态提示注入攻击，通过仅注入图像来同时操控LVLM对文本和视觉输入的解读，解决了现有攻击无法实现跨模态提示扰动的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM提示注入攻击存在关键局限：注入的提示只能操控单一模态的模型解读，或虽是跨模态但无法实现真正的跨模态提示扰动。随着LVLM部署增加，攻击面扩大，亟需更强大的跨模态攻击方法。

Method: 1) 将注入提示扰动优化从视觉嵌入空间（10^5参数）转向模型隐藏状态空间（10^7参数）; 2) 提出层选择策略，识别对多模态融合最关键的层（发现中间层而非最后层最优）; 3) 提出距离递减扰动预算分配策略，根据像素到语义关键区域的距离递减分配预算。

Result: 在多个LVLM和数据集上的实验表明，CrossMPI显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CrossMPI实现了真正意义上的跨模态提示注入，通过图像单模态注入即可同时扰动文本和视觉模态的模型解读，揭示了LVLM在安全防护上的新漏洞。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for multimodal intelligence, but their growing deployment also expands the attack surface of prompt injection. Despite this growing concern, existing attacks still suffer from a critical limitation: the injected prompt for one modality only steers the model's interpretation of that singular input. Alternatively, these attacks remain multimodal but fail to achieve cross-modal prompt perturbation. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel cross-modal prompt injection attack CrossMPI, which can steer the model's interpretation of both textual and visual inputs via image-only prompt injection. Our design is underpinned by the following key breakthroughs. First, we turn the focus of the injected prompt perturbation optimization from the visual embedding space (typically with only $10^5$ parameters) to the model hidden state space (for multimodal information integration and with $10^7$ parameters). Then, two strategies are adopted to mitigate the optimization challenges posed by the larger parameter space. To constrain the optimized model parameter space, we introduce a layer selection strategy that identifies the layers most critical to multimodal integration. Interestingly, deviating from the past experience, our analysis reveals that the optimal layers for LVLM prompt perturbation reside in the middle of the model rather than the last. To constrain the image perturbation space, we propose a new distance-decremental perturbation budget assignment strategy that allocates budgets decrementally as the pixel distance to semantic-critical regions increases. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and datasets show that our method significantly outperforms baseline approaches.

</details>


### [123] [PCDM: A Diffusion-Based Data Poisoning Attack Against Federated Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16098)
*Wei Sun,Yijun Chen,Bo Gao,Ke Xiong,Yuwei Wang,Pingyi Fan,Khaled Ben Letaief*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型（PCDM）的数据投毒框架，用于攻击联邦学习系统。该框架通过可调节的投毒向量和跳跃扩散策略，实现了对本地生成投毒数据的细粒度控制，在保证攻击有效性和隐蔽性的同时，有效规避了现有防御机制（如拜占庭鲁棒聚合）。实验表明，PCDM在多个数据集上比现有GAN-based方法更具隐蔽性和破坏性，对联邦学习数据安全构成重大威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习因其分布式特性易受数据投毒攻击。现有基于GAN的数据投毒方法虽能生成看似合法的投毒数据，但GAN输出的固有一致性仍会暴露投毒痕迹。因此需要一种更隐蔽且可控的投毒方法。

Method: 提出投毒导向条件扩散模型（PCDM）：(1) 在全局上下文中引入可调节的投毒向量，实现对投毒数据生成的精确控制；(2) 采用跳跃扩散策略实现轻量高效的投毒数据生成；(3) 提供攻击性能的理论保证。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和VRAI五个数据集上，PCDM相比SOTA方法更不易表现出统计异常，同时更有效地降低全局联邦学习性能，可绕过先进的拜占庭鲁棒聚合防御机制。

Conclusion: PCDM框架在攻击有效性和隐蔽性方面均优于现有方法，对联邦学习的数据安全构成显著风险，揭示了扩散模型在数据投毒攻击中的潜在威胁。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to data poisoning attacks due to its distributed nature. Although recent GAN-based data poisoning methods have indicated the potential of using generative AI to generate seemingly legitimate poisoned data, the inherent consistency of GAN outputs can still reveal a sign of data poisoning. In this paper, we propose a diffusion-based data poisoning framework against FL systems, which leverages a Poisoning-Oriented Conditional Diffusion Model (PCDM) to enable fine-grained control over the local generation of poisoned data while ensuring both attack effectiveness and stealthiness. Our PCDM incorporates an adjustable poisoning vector within the global context to precisely control the generation of poisoned data, with theoretical guarantees on attack performance. Furthermore, it employs a novel jumping diffusion strategy for lightweight and efficient poisoned data generation. We conduct the most systematic and broad experimental evaluation for FL poisoning attacks against various defenses, including advanced Byzantine robust aggregation mechanisms, on four open datasets: MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a real-world wireless-specific dataset VRAI. Our results demonstrate that PCDM is less likely to exhibit statistical anomalies compared with the state-of-the-art methods while more effectively degrading global FL performance, which poses a significant risk to data security in FL.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [On Kernel Eigen-alignments of KRR: Reconstruction and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15240)
*Yang Liu,Ernest Fokoue,Richard Lange,Daniel Krutz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了核矩阵特征对齐与学习目标在实现鲁棒泛化中的关键作用，通过特征值和特征向量估计视角建立了泛化误差界，发现高秩核可轻易实现近零重构误差但对泛化无预测力，并揭示了强泛化需要特征向量对齐、特征值幅度或特征值间隔的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在泛化性能分析上不够直观或假设过强，本文旨在建立核方法与矩阵特征估计之间的直接联系，从而更直观地理解泛化行为。

Method: 通过分析核矩阵扰动对特征向量/特征值估计稳定性的影响，推导核岭回归(KRR)的泛化误差界；在有限样本设定下，从特征值/特征向量估计角度研究由次优有限训练集引起的泛化误差。

Result: 高秩核可轻松获得近零重构误差，但重构误差对泛化预测能力有限；建立了基于特征值/特征向量估计的泛化界，表明强泛化需要更大的特征向量对齐、特征值幅度或特征值间隔。

Conclusion: 泛化性能依赖于核矩阵与学习目标之间的特征对齐程度，以及特征值分布特性，而非单纯的重构误差最小化。

Abstract: This paper investigates the critical role of eigenalignments between the kernel matrix and learning targets in achieving robust generalization in learning problems. We establish a direct connection between generalization performance in kernel methods and the estimation of eigenvectors and eigenvalues of matrices, offering a more intuitive understanding compared to prior work with minimal assumptions. We also show that, since the prediction task in KRR is essentially the weighted sum of eigenvectors/singular vectors, by analyzing how much error can be caused by perturbations to the kernel matrix, we can then derive a bound on this generalization error using the estimation stability of matrix eigenvalues and eigenvectors. Compared with previous work, our analysis concentrates on finite-sample settings and on the generalization error arising from having a suboptimal finite training set. Our findings reveal that in kernel methods, as long as the kernel is of high rank, the near-zero reconstruction error can be trivially obtained, implying that the reconstruction error will have limited predictive power for generalization. Finally, we establish a generalization bound from an eigenvalues/eigenvectors estimation perspective, showing that strong generalization requires increasing eigenvector alignment, eigenvalue magnitude, or gaps between consecutive eigenvalues.

</details>


### [125] [Harnessing Unimodality in Semiparametric Contextual Pricing via Oracle Price Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15411)
*Yingying Fan,Yuxuan Han,Jinchi Lv,Xiaocong Xu,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究半参数标量指数估值模型下的上下文动态定价问题。在该模型中，潜在价值由未知效用映射和未知噪声分布决定，关键决策对象是由标量指数和噪声尾部诱导的一维最优价格映射。在噪声尾部函数满足β≥2阶Hölder光滑性及收益几何条件（保证唯一、稳定、内点最大化）下，该最优价格映射具有(β-1)阶光滑性。作者提出ORBIT策略——一种模块化的由粗到精的定价方法，以标量引导指数为输入，在每个活跃区间内定位基准价格，通过置信域内的带状凸优化学习最优价格映射的局部多项式逼近。对于线性效用模型，自适应椭圆探索方案无需对上下文分布做假设即可在线构建所需标量引导。该策略实现了Õ(T^{(2β-1)/(4β-3)} + √(dT))的遗憾界。对于固定d，作者建立了匹配的下界，揭示非参数最优价格映射学习项达到极小化最优。同一标量引导接口还可扩展至高维稀疏线性效用和非参数Hölder效用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文动态定价中，现有工作多假设估值函数形式已知（如线性），或对噪声分布有强假设。实际场景中估值函数和噪声分布均未知，且高维上下文导致维度灾难。本文动机是设计一种能适应未知半参数结构、利用潜在低维结构（标量指数）来实现高效定价的策略，同时最小化遗憾。

Method: 提出ORBIT策略，模块化由粗到精：1）以标量引导指数（如线性效用中的估计指数）为输入；2）将价格空间划分为活跃区间（bins），在每个区间内定位基准价格；3）在置信域内通过带状凸优化学习最优价格映射的局部多项式逼近。对于线性效用模型，采用自适应椭圆探索在线构建标量引导指数，无需上下文分布假设。理论分析利用最优价格映射的(β-1)阶光滑性进行非参数学习。

Result: ORBIT策略在β-Hölder噪声尾部条件下达到Õ(T^{(2β-1)/(4β-3)} + √(dT))遗憾界。对于固定d，建立了匹配的遗憾下界，证明非参数最优价格映射学习项是极小化最优的。此外，该方法可扩展至稀疏高维线性和非参数Hölder效用模型。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了上下文动态定价中潜在的低维结构（标量指数最优价格映射的光滑性）可以被有效利用，通过模块化设计分离参数估计与价格决策，实现最优遗憾。标量引导接口的设计具有良好可扩展性，为更复杂的效用模型提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing in a semiparametric scalar-index valuation model where the latent value is $v_t=μ_\ast(\mathsf c_t)+ξ_t$, with an unknown utility map $μ_\ast$ and an unknown additive noise distribution. The key decision object is the one-dimensional oracle price map $u\mapsto p^\ast(u)$ induced by the scalar index $u=μ_\ast(\mathsf c)$ and the noise tail. Under the $β$-Hölder smoothness of the tail function for $β\geq 2$ and a revenue-geometry condition that gives a unique, stable, interior maximizer, this oracle map is itself $(β-1)$-smooth. We exploit such structure through $\mathsf{ORBIT}$, a modular coarse-to-fine policy that takes a scalar pilot index as input, localizes a benchmark price in each active bin, and learns a local polynomial approximation of the oracle map inside a trust region via bandit convex optimization. For the baseline linear utility model $μ_\ast(\mathsf c)=\mathsf c^\topθ_\ast$, an adaptive elliptical exploration scheme constructs the required scalar pilot online without distributional assumptions on the contexts. The resulting policy achieves regret $\widetilde{O}\big(T^{\frac{2β-1}{4β-3}}+\sqrt{dT}\big)$. For fixed $d$, we establish a matching lower bound in the horizon dependence, unveiling that the nonparametric oracle-map learning term is minimax sharp. The same scalar-pilot interface also yields extensions to sparse high-dimensional linear utility and nonparametric Hölder utility.

</details>


### [126] [MaxSketch: Robust Distinct Counting in Streams via Random Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15571)
*Nikos Tsikouras,Constantine Caramanis,Christos Tzamos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 MaxSketch，一种基于随机高斯投影的 max-linear 草图，用于在高维噪声流中估计不同对象的数量。经典算法 HyperLogLog 因依赖相同元素的哈希一致性而在近似重复场景失效。作者证明，当数据具有常见于学习表示中的几何结构时，仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ 个随机投影（即 $\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ 内存）即可实现 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 因子内的精确估计，显著优于一般度量空间中 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{n})$ 的最坏情况最优界。实验表明该方法在图像流上准确估计不同计数并能泛化至训练分布之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典流式计数算法（如 HyperLogLog）假设重复元素完全相同，但在现代高维噪声场景中（如不同图像中的同一人脸），重复对象仅近似相似，导致哈希一致性失效。尽管已有工作在一般度量空间中实现了鲁棒不同元素计数，但其 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{n})$ 的最坏情况内存界限制了实际应用。作者希望利用常见于学习表示中的几何结构来突破这一瓶颈，实现更高效的不同计数。

Method: 提出 MaxSketch 草图，采用随机高斯投影构建 max-linear 结构。对每个数据点，计算其与多个随机高斯向量的内积，并取最大值作为哈希签名；通过维护这些最大值的统计信息来估计不同元素数量。理论分析证明，在数据具有几何结构（如低维子空间或聚类结构）的假设下，$\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ 个投影足以实现高精度估计。

Result: 理论证明：在几何结构假设下，MaxSketch 仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ 内存即可实现 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 因子内的不同计数估计，而一般度量空间的最坏情况界为 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{n})$。实验结果表明，MaxSketch 在图像流数据集上准确估计不同元素数量，并且能够泛化到训练分布之外的场景。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了经典流式算法与现代表示学习之间的鸿沟，揭示了数据几何结构可以根本上降低不同元素计数问题的复杂度。MaxSketch 为高维噪声流数据的高效不同计数提供了实用且理论保证的方案。

Abstract: Estimating the number of distinct elements in a data stream is well understood when repeated elements are identical. In modern settings, however, observations are high-dimensional and noisy, so repeated instances of the same object are only approximately similar -- for example, different images of the same individual may vary significantly at the pixel level. Classical sketches such as HyperLogLog rely on consistent hash values for identical elements and break down in this regime. Recent work on robust distinct counting in general metric spaces achieves $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{n})$ memory, which is tight in the worst case. We show that substantially improved memory guarantees are possible under geometric structure common in learned representations. We introduce MaxSketch, a simple max-linear sketch built from random Gaussian projections, and prove that it succeeds in estimating the number of distinct latent objects. Concretely, we show that under this assumption $m = \widetilde{O} (\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ random projections (and hence $\widetilde{O} (\log n/\varepsilon^2)$ memory) suffice to recover the true distinct count within a $(1+\varepsilon)$ factor. Experiments on image streams confirm that MaxSketch accurately estimates distinct counts and generalizes beyond the training regime. Our results bridge classical streaming algorithms and modern representation learning, showing how geometric structure can fundamentally reduce the complexity of distinct counting.

</details>


### [127] [Pessimistic Risk-Aware Policy Learning in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15620)
*Yilong Wan,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究离线情境下的风险感知策略学习问题，提出了一个统一分布框架用于优化Lipschitz连续风险泛函（包括均值-方差、熵风险、条件风险价值等），通过开发新的经验浓度不等式，实现了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$率的数据依赖次优性界，该率达到极小极大最优，且与风险中性离线策略优化的统计代价相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗、金融）中，在线交互不可行，且必须严格控制不良结果。现有离线上下文赌博机文献要么聚焦于期望奖励标准（风险中性），要么将风险考量限制在策略评估而非优化上。因此需要一套统一框架来支持离线情境下对广泛风险准则的策略优化。

Method: 提出一个统一的分布式框架，将优化目标设定为Lipschitz连续风险泛函；开发了基于重要性采样的分布估计器的新型经验浓度不等式；推导出数据依赖的次优性界，其收敛率为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$，且无需严格的均匀重叠假设。

Result: 得到了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$率的次优性界，该界是极小极大最优的，且与风险中性（期望奖励）离线策略优化的统计率相匹配，表明优化一般Lipschitz风险准则相较于期望奖励不产生额外的统计代价。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了离线策略优化中风险中性与风险感知方法之间的差距，证明了在无需额外统计成本的前提下，离线情境下可以高效地优化一大类广泛的风险函数。

Abstract: We study risk-aware offline policy learning, aiming to learn a decision rule from logged data that is optimal under general risk criteria. This problem is crucial in high-stakes domains where online interaction is infeasible and adverse outcomes must be carefully controlled. However, existing literature on offline contextual bandits either centers on expected-reward criteria or restricts risk considerations to policy evaluation instead of optimization. In this work, we propose a unified distributional framework for optimizing Lipschitz-continuous risk functionals, a broad class of risk measures encompassing mean-variance, entropic risk, and conditional value-at-risk, among others. By developing novel empirical concentration inequalities for importance sampling-based distributional estimators, our analysis derives data-dependent suboptimality bounds with an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ rate, without relying on restrictive uniform overlap assumptions. This rate is minimax optimal and matches that of risk-neutral offline policy optimization, indicating that optimizing general Lipschitz risk criteria incurs no additional statistical cost relative to the expected-reward.

</details>


### [128] [$α$-TCAV: A Unified Framework for Testing with Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15688)
*Ekkehard Schnoor,Jawher Said,Malik Tiomoko,Wojciech Samek,Alexander Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了概念激活向量（CAV）的统计不稳定性问题，揭示了标准TCAV评分中指示函数导致的方差不衰减缺陷，并提出了α-TCAV框架，通过参数化平滑函数替代指示函数，统一了TCAV和Multi-TCAV。理论分析还给出了最优参数选择指南，并挑战了现有实践：建议将全部采样预算分配给单个CAV而非多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念激活向量（CAV）是深度学习可解释性的基础工具，但存在统计不稳定性问题，限制了其实用性。现有TCAV方法在关键场景下存在方差不衰减的根本缺陷，亟需理论分析和改进方案。

Method: 首先从理论层面推导了PatternCAV、FastCAV和岭回归CAV等主要CAV类别的分布特性；然后通过分析标准TCAV评分的统计性质，发现其指示函数在临界区域导致非衰减方差；最后提出α-TCAV框架，用参数化平滑函数替代不连续的指示函数，并给出参数选择的理论指导。

Result: 建立了统一的概率化TCAV公式，证明现有最先进方法缺乏理论依据；给出了α-TCAV参数调优的准则（模拟Multi-TCAV或得到贝叶斯最优度量）；提出了挑战传统做法的建议：将全部采样预算集中用于单个CAV而非分散到多个CAV。

Conclusion: α-TCAV框架从理论上解决了CAV的统计不稳定性问题，提供了更具原则性的概念解释方法，其结论对现有可解释性研究中的常见实践（如多CAV采样策略）提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) are a fundamental tool for concept-based explainability in deep learning, yet their practical utility is limited by statistical instability. We analyze the stochastic nature of CAVs and the Testing with CAVs (TCAV) method, deriving the distributions of major CAV classes including PatternCAV, FastCAV, and ridge regression-based CAVs. We then identify a fundamental flaw in the standard TCAV score: its reliance on a discontinuous indicator function induces non-decaying variance in critical regimes. To address this, we introduce $α$-TCAV, a generalized framework that replaces the indicator with a parameterized smooth function, yielding a unified probabilistic formulation that subsumes both TCAV and Multi-TCAV. We characterize the induced distributions of sensitivity scores and different TCAV variants, showing that established state-of-the-art choices lack theoretical justification. We provide principled guidance on tuning the parameter in $α$-TCAV -- either to imitate Multi-TCAV at substantially lower computational cost, or to obtain a calibrated Bayes-optimal probabilistic measure of a concept's influence. Finally, our analysis yields practical recommendations that challenge established routines: most notably, allocating the full sampling budget to a single CAV rather than splitting it across several.

</details>


### [129] [Unsupervised Domain Shift Detection with Interpretable Subspace Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15920)
*Sebastian Springer,Alessandro Laio*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种检测高维特征空间中局部密度异常（域偏移）的工具，能够识别偏移的特征子空间并补偿偏移，在20维基准和782维心电图上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集中概率分布的微妙差异（域偏移）可能导致下游模型产生偏差，需要一种可解释的检测和补偿方法。

Method: 利用局部密度异常检测算法识别高维特征空间中的域偏移，通过寻找异常最显著的特征子空间实现可解释性，并通过从未标注数据中提取无分布差异的子集进行偏移补偿。

Result: 在20维可控基准上正确恢复了广域和局部偏移及其支持特征子空间；在782维心电图中检测到设备引起的偏移，提取了富含不平衡设备成分的代表性子集，并识别了与采集差异相关的ECG特征。

Conclusion: 密度偏移检测与子空间归因为在建模前揭示隐藏的队列偏差提供了实用框架。

Abstract: We developed a tool for detecting domain shifts, namely subtle differences in the probability distributions of datasets. We identify these shifts using an algorithm designed to detect localised density anomalies in high-dimensional feature spaces. If an anomaly is present, we then identify the feature subspace in which the anomaly is most pronounced. This allows us to trace the domain shift to a small set of features, making the shift interpretable. Moreover, we provide a protocol for compensating domain shifts by extracting, from two unlabelled datasets, subsets of samples with no detectable residual distributional difference. We validate the framework on controlled 20-dimensional benchmarks with known ground truth, recovering both broad and localized shifts together with their supporting feature subspaces. We then apply it to healthy electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings represented by 782 features. In age- and sex-matched cohort comparisons differing in measurement-device composition, the method detects device-induced shifts, extracts representative subsets enriched in the imbalanced device components, and identifies ECG features associated with the acquisition contrast. These results suggest that density-shift detection and subspace attribution provide a practical framework for uncovering hidden cohort biases before downstream modelling.

</details>


### [130] [Testing properties of trees in graphical models with covariance queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15996)
*Sofiya Burova,Francisco Calvillo,Gábor Lugosi,Piotr Zwiernik*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维图模型下树结构图的全局性质测试问题，基于协方差查询模型，设计了针对叶子数、最大度、典型距离和直径等全局性质的高效随机化测试程序，实现了次二次查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图模型背景下，完整重建树结构代价高昂，但某些全局结构性质可以通过更少的查询高效测试，因此研究如何利用协方差查询高效检验树结构的全局性质。

Method: 采用Lugosi等人提出的协方差查询模型，针对树结构图设计随机化测试算法，分别对叶子数、最大度、典型距离和直径等全局性质构造测试程序，并分析各性质的查询复杂度界限。

Result: 证明了对于树结构图的多个全局性质，可以在次二次查询复杂度内完成高效测试，并给出了各性质依赖于目标阈值和容忍度参数的显式查询复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 树结构图的全局性质测试可以用远少于完整重建的查询次数高效完成，为高维图模型下稀疏图的性质测试提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of testing properties of graphs underlying high-dimensional graphical models. We adopt the model of covariance queries introduced by Lugosi, Truszkowski, Velona, and Zwiernik (2021). We study the case when the underlying graph is a tree. The main results of the paper show that, while reconstructing the entire tree may be costly, certain global structural properties can be tested efficiently. In particular, we design randomized tests for global structural properties that use a sub-quadratic number of queries. We develop testing procedures for several fundamental properties, including the number of leaves, the maximum degree, the typical distance, and the diameter of the tree. For each property, we obtain explicit query complexity bounds that depend on the target threshold and tolerance parameters.

</details>


### [131] [Explainable AI Isn't Enough! Rethinking Algorithmic Contestability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16041)
*Timo Freiesleben,Kristof Meding,Gunnar König*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文指出算法可争议性（algorithmic contestability）作为算法追责的重要维度长期被忽视，提出了其操作化定义，识别了三种可使决策被推翻的证据类型（预测多重性、错误特征值、被忽略的推翻性证据），并论证了现有欧盟法律已赋予个体获取这些证据的部分权利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管XAI已大量研究算法追索（recourse），但帮助个体审查和纠正错误算法决策的算法可争议性问题缺乏形式化定义和系统性操作化，而其具有重要的伦理和法律意义。

Method: 首先提出可争议性作为追索的互补概念的操作化定义；然后分析标准XAI解释（反事实、LIME、Anchors）的局限性；进而识别三类可推翻决策的证据类型；最后分析欧盟立法框架与所提框架的对应关系。

Result: 标准XAI解释即使结合人类关于决策连续性或单调性的直觉，也只能揭示个体邻域内的错误，不足以推翻决策。识别出三种符合决策者自身伦理标准、可使决策在规范上不可辩护的证据：预测多重性、错误特征值、被忽略的推翻性证据。

Conclusion: 算法可争议性应与追索并重，个体已拥有获取上述证据的部分法律权利，需进一步推动该方向的研究与实践。

Abstract: Machine learning systems increasingly make life-changing decisions about individuals, such as loan approvals, hiring, and cheating detection, raising a pressing question: how can individuals respond to negative decisions made by these opaque systems? While explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has largely focused on algorithmic recourse -- helping individuals change their features to obtain a desired outcome -- the parallel problem of algorithmic contestability -- helping individuals review and correct erroneous algorithmic decisions -- has received far less attention, despite its central ethical and legal importance. We trace this neglect to the absence of clear formal definitions and a systematic operationalization of contestability as an algorithmic problem. To address it, we propose an operational definition of contestability as a natural complement to recourse: contestability starts from the presumption that a decision may be incorrect and focuses on identifying evidence to challenge and potentially overturn it, whereas recourse assumes the decision is valid and instead provides pathways for changing it. We show that standard XAI explanations, such as counterfactuals, LIME, or Anchors, even when combined with human intuitions about decision continuity or monotonicity, reveal only errors in the neighborhood of the individual, but provide insufficient grounds for overturning the decision at hand. Going thus beyond traditional XAI, we identify three types of evidence warranting reversal according to the decision maker's own ethical standards: predictive multiplicity, incorrect feature values, and neglected overruling evidence. We argue that these render decisions normatively indefensible and thus successfully contestable. Finally, we analyze how existing EU legislation connects to our framework and argue that individuals already hold some legal rights to these forms of evidence.

</details>


### [132] [Skew-adaptive conformal prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16145)
*Paulo C. Marques F.,Helton Graziadei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种偏斜自适应分裂保形预测方法，通过非对称区间族和逆双曲正弦变换学习预测不确定性的偏斜特征，在保持有限样本边际有效性的同时生成自适应局部尺度和偏斜的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统保形预测区间通常对称，无法捕捉预测分布中的偏斜特征，导致区间效率低下。作者希望开发一种能自适应数据局部偏斜特性的方法，提高预测区间的效率。

Method: 基于非对称区间族和规范评分方法推导一致性评分；使用逆双曲正弦变换处理符号缩放残差作为训练目标；通过额外预测模型学习特征空间中预测不确定性的偏斜倾向；保留分裂保形预测的有限样本边际有效性。

Result: 在多种数据集上的实验表明，该方法在预测区间效率上优于缩放评分构造和保形分位数回归；提出的估计器能准确匹配测试样本上的平均宽度比。

Conclusion: 偏斜自适应方法在保持保形预测有效性的前提下，显著提升了预测区间的效率，为处理非对称预测分布提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We develop a skew-adaptive extension of split conformal prediction for regression. The method starts from an asymmetric interval family centered at a point prediction and uses the gauge approach to deduce the conformity score induced by this family. The inverse hyperbolic sine transform of signed scaled residuals provides the training target for an additional predictive model, whose role is to learn how predictive uncertainty should tilt across the feature space. The resulting procedure preserves the finite-sample marginal validity of split conformal prediction under exchangeability, while producing intervals that adapt to both local scale and local skewness. We also develop a calibration-sample-based estimator for comparing the expected relative future width of the skew-adaptive and classical scaled-score intervals. Experiments on a variety of datasets indicate gains in prediction interval efficiency over the scaled-score construction and conformalized quantile regression, and show that the proposed estimator closely matches the corresponding average width ratio observed on the test sample.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Node-private community estimation in stochastic block models: Tractable algorithms and lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15943)
*Laurentiu Marchis,Ethan D'souza,Tomáš Flídr,Po-Ling Loh*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机块模型中社区恢复问题的节点差分隐私变体，开发了基于谱聚类的多种私有化算法，并提出了通过Lipschitz扩展的指数机制采样和构造有界度图平滑投影等新方法，同时建立了节点隐私下社区一致估计所需ε增长率的理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典社区恢复问题缺乏对节点级图结构变化的稳定性保证（即差分隐私约束），而现有的边缘隐私算法直接应用于节点隐私场景会导致隐私参数ε急剧增加，无法实现一致估计。

Method: 提出了两类新算法：(1) 基于Lipschitz扩展的指数机制采样；(2) 从无向图空间到有界度图空间的光滑投影框架，可结合多种边缘隐私算法。同时还采用了私有化邻接矩阵、私有PCA、私有凸优化、私有低秩矩阵估计和私有近似子空间估计等方法。

Result: 所有方法在节点数多项式时间内可计算；建立了节点隐私下一致社区估计所需ε增长率的理论下界；揭示了非标准标度ε→∞下私有算法分析的复杂性；提出了HGR最大相关性在PAC学习精度放大中的新应用。

Conclusion: 节点差分隐私下的社区恢复问题比边缘隐私更具挑战性，但本文提出的新算法能够通过多项式时间计算实现一致估计，同时提供了相应的信息论下界。

Abstract: We study the classical problem of community recovery in stochastic block models with a fixed number of communities, with a twist: We seek algorithms that are stable with respect to node-wise changes in the graph structure, formally defined as a differential privacy constraint. The algorithms we develop are based on spectral clustering, where we introduce privacy to the community recovery pipeline in the form of directly privatizing the adjacency matrix; private PCA; private convex optimization; private low-rank matrix estimation; and private approximate subspace estimation. Straightforward applications of existing private algorithms lead to a rapid increase in the privacy parameter $ε$ in order to ensure consistent estimation under node differential privacy, in contrast with the simpler setting of edge privacy. To alleviate these issues, we develop novel algorithms based on (1) sampling from an exponential mechanism with a Lipschitz extension and (2) a general framework for constructing smooth projections from the space of undirected graphs to the space of bounded-degree graphs, which can then be combined with various edge-private algorithms. Importantly, the methods we develop are all computable in polynomial-time as a function of the number of nodes in the graph. We also develop novel lower bounds on the growth rate of $ε$ required in order to achieve consistent community estimation under node privacy. On a technical note, our paper highlights the complications that arise when analyzing private algorithms under the non-standard scaling $ε\rightarrow \infty$ and proposes some solutions. We also provide a novel application of the HGR maximal correlation from information theory in the context of accuracy amplification in PAC learning, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [134] [Intrinsic-dimension empirical Bernstein inequalities for bounded self-adjoint operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15278)
*Diego Martinez-Taboada,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了首个完全数据驱动的Bennett和Bernstein不等式，适用于独立有界紧自伴随机算子之和。这些基于经验方差的界用内蕴维度替代环境维度，产生可计算、无维度的保证，且在非各向同性随机矩阵和无限维希尔伯特空间中严格优于经典oracle界。经验界渐近达到最优oracle速率，并附带内蕴维度本身的经验集中保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典算子值集中不等式存在两个关键局限：(1) 需要预先知道真实的方差（oracle界），这在实践中往往不可得；(2) 界显式地依赖于环境维度，对于无限维算子或具有高度结构化的高维算子（如低秩矩阵）而言失效或过于保守。

Method: 利用经验方差替代真实方差，建立完全数据驱动的Bennett和Bernstein型不等式。通过将界表达为内蕴维度（如有效秩或迹）而非环境维度的函数，实现无维度的集中保证。关键技术包括对随机算子矩生成函数的精细控制和经验谱分析的集中工具。

Result: (1) 得到首个经验Bennett和Bernstein不等式，适用于独立有界紧自伴算子之和；(2) 这些界是数据驱动的、可计算的，且不依赖于环境维度；(3) 对于非各向同性随机矩阵，严格优于现有oracle界；(4) 可无缝推广到无限维希尔伯特空间；(5) 达到与最优oracle界相同的渐近速率；(6) 导出了内蕴维度本身的经验集中不等式。

Conclusion: 本文填补了算子值集中不等式理论中数据驱动界的空白。经验Bennett/Bernstein不等式将经典理论从依赖先验方差和环境维度推进到适应数据且依赖内蕴维度的新范式，为高维统计、随机算法和无限维问题提供了更实用且更紧的工具。内蕴维度的经验保证也构成了独立的贡献。

Abstract: Operator-valued concentration inequalities are foundational to the analysis of modern high-dimensional statistics and randomized algorithms. However, standard oracle bounds are frequently limited in practice: they require explicit a priori knowledge of the true variance, and often explicitly scale with the ambient dimension, rendering them vacuous for infinite-dimensional or heavily structured operators. Motivated by these challenges, we establish the first empirical Bennett and Bernstein inequalities for sums of independent, bounded, compact self-adjoint operators. Our fully data-driven bounds replace the unknown variance with an empirical estimate and rely strictly on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. This structural shift yields computable, dimension-free guarantees that are strictly sharper for non-isotropic random matrices and seamlessly extend to infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces. We demonstrate that our empirical bounds achieve asymptotic sharpness with the best known oracle rates. Finally, as an independent byproduct, we derive novel empirical concentration guarantees for the intrinsic dimension itself.

</details>


### [135] [Bounds on the Number of Modes of a Gaussian Mixture Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15531)
*Hien Duy Nguyen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对k组分高斯混合密度的临界点（尤其是模态）数量给出了显式的上下界。通过Pfaffian方法、代数增强和Morse理论，得到了异方差和同方差情形下的上界；同时通过二项式组合、填充乘积和种子闭包原理构造了下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯混合模型是统计学习与数据分析中的基本工具，其模态（mode）数量直接影响聚类解释和密度估计的拓扑性质。然而，对于一般k组分高斯混合，临界点（包括模态、鞍点等）的精确计数长期缺乏严格的理论界限。该工作旨在填补这一空白，为高斯混合的几何复杂性提供显式组合上界和下界。

Method: 1) 通过参考分量归一化临界点方程，结合Pfaffian技术得到直接上界U_het；2) 引入代数增强（augmented reciprocal variable）获得替代上界U_aug；3) 利用Morse理论将临界点上界转化为模态上界；4) 对同方差情形，利用仿射秩约简和增强同方差约简得到更紧的维度无关上界U_aug,hom；5) 下界通过二项式组合构造L_bin，结合填充-乘积（padding-product）和种子闭包（seed-closure）原理生成更丰富的下界族。

Result: 异方差情形下临界点上界为min{U_het, U_aug}，模态上界取该值整除2后下取整；同方差情形下U_hom更紧，且U_aug,hom达到维度无关形式。下界L_bin = k + max_{2≤r≤min(d,k)} C(k,r)，线性下界d+k-1由填充-乘积族保证。

Conclusion: 为高斯混合的临界点数量提供了首个系统的显式组合界限，连接了代数和拓扑方法，对理解混合模型几何结构具有理论意义，并可能指导聚类算法中初始分量数的选择。

Abstract: We derive explicit upper bounds for the number of nondegenerate critical points of a $k$-component Gaussian mixture density in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and the number of modes when the modal set is finite, together with lower bounds. By normalizing the critical-point equations by a reference component, for $k\ge2$ we get the direct Pfaffian bound \[ U_{\mathrm{het}}(d,k)=2^{\,d+\binom{k-1}{2}}\left(d+2\min(d,k-1)+1\right)^{k-1}. \] For the same parameter range, an exact elimination augmented by an algebraic reciprocal variable gives the alternative bound \[ U_{\mathrm{aug}}(d,k)= 2^{\binom{k-1}{2}}(d+1)\left((2k-1)d+2k-1\right)^{k-1}. \] Thus, for $k\ge2$, the best critical-point bound is their minimum. A Morse-theoretic argument improves the corresponding finite-mode upper bound to \[ \left\lfloor \frac{\min\{U_{\mathrm{het}}(d,k),U_{\mathrm{aug}}(d,k)\}+1}{2}\right\rfloor. \] In the homoscedastic case, for $k\ge2$, the direct bound improves to \[ U_{\mathrm{hom}}(d,k)=2^{\,d+\binom{k-1}{2}}\left(d+\min(d,k-1)+1\right)^{k-1}, \] an affine-rank reduction replaces $d$ by the affine rank of the component means, and an augmented homoscedastic reduction gives the dimension-free bound \[ U_{\mathrm{aug,hom}}(k)=2^{\binom{k-1}{2}+1}(2k)^{k-1}. \] On the lower-bound side, for $d,k\ge 2$ we obtain \[ L_{\mathrm{bin}}(d,k)=k+\max_{2\le r\le \min(d,k)}\binom{k}{r}, \] together with a padding-product family that in particular implies the linear lower bound $d+k-1$, and a seed-closure principle that packages product and padding constructions. We further give explicit bounds for the number of connected components of the critical set.

</details>


### [136] [Goodness-of-Fit Testing for Point Processes in Large Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15814)
*Sami Umut Can,Estate V. Khmaladze,Roger J. A. Laeven*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对参数化点过程的条件强度函数进行拟合优度检验的新方法。通过构造参数检验过程的酉变换，使其弱收敛于一个与零假设下参数形式无关的“标准”目标过程，从而实现渐近分布无关的检验。通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Aalen生存过程、混合治愈模型、软件可靠性模型）和真实数据（人类寿命、软件故障）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化点过程的拟合优度检验方法通常依赖于特定的参数假设形式，缺乏统一且渐近分布无关的检验框架。作者希望克服这一局限，提出一种不依赖于零假设下具体参数形式的通用检验方法。

Method: 构造一个“参数检验过程”（parametric testing process），然后对其施加一个酉变换（unitary transformation），使得变换后的过程弱收敛于一个标准的目标过程（如布朗桥或Wiener过程）。该变换的设计使得极限分布与零假设下的具体参数形式无关，从而实现渐近分布无关（asymptotically distribution-free）的检验。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明，该方法在Aalen型生存过程（有无删失）、混合治愈模型和软件可靠性模型中，有限样本表现良好。真实数据（人类寿命和软件故障）上的应用进一步验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的酉变换方法为参数化点过程的拟合优度检验提供了一种统一的、渐近分布无关的解决方案，具有良好的有限样本性能和广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Suppose we have an observed path from a point process counting event occurrences in a large population. Based on the observed path, we would like to test the null hypothesis that the conditional intensity of the point process belongs to a particular parametric family. We propose a novel approach to conducting such goodness-of-fit tests. The idea is to construct a unitary transformation of a natural parametric testing process such that it converges weakly to a ``standard'' target process, independent of the particular parametric form assumed under the null hypothesis. This transformation therefore paves the way for asymptotically distribution-free goodness-of-fit testing of parametric point processes. We demonstrate the good finite-sample performance of our approach through Monte Carlo simulations of Aalen-type survival processes, without and with censoring, mixture cure models, and software reliability models, and we illustrate its applicability with observed human lifetimes as well as real software failures.

</details>


### [137] [Nearest-Neighbour Matching on Unbounded Supports and Covariate Shift Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16027)
*Simon Viel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在缺失标签的多变量函数期望估计问题，证明了基于最近邻匹配的非参数估计器在极弱协变量支撑假设下仍能达到通常收敛速率，并将经典理论推广到流形支撑和重尾目标分布等情况，同时揭示了迁移性度量对估计速率的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于最近邻匹配的非参数估计文献通常假设协变量位于$\R^d$中形状良好的紧致子集上，且密度有正下界。本文旨在放松这些强假设，在更一般的协变量支撑条件下建立收敛速率理论。

Method: 通过引入源分布与目标分布之间的迁移性度量（measure of transferability）替代传统的协变量支撑假设，在流形支撑、重尾目标分布等更一般场景下推导最近邻匹配估计量的收敛速率。

Result: 证明了在最小化的协变量支撑假设下，通常的收敛速率仍可达到；同时证明了迁移性控制是任何估计量实现良好收敛速率的必要条件。应用于治疗效应估计时，可放松倾向得分须有界远离0和1的经典假设。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失标签多变量函数期望的最近邻匹配估计提供了更普适的理论框架，显著放宽了经典假设条件，并揭示了迁移性这一核心概念在理论中的必要性。

Abstract: Expectations of multivariate functions with missing labels occur in various fields such as transfer learning and average treatment effects. Although non-parametric estimators based on nearest-neighbour matching are frequently used in this context, the existing literature assumes that the covariates live in some well-shaped compact subset of $\R^d$, with densities that are bounded away from zero. In this paper, we show that the usual rates of convergence can be achieved with minimal assumptions on the covariate supports. These assumptions are replaced with conditions on the source and target distributions, among which a measure of the tranferability between the two probability measures. We show that these conditions are general, can be applied to distributions supported on manifolds, and allow the target distribution to have a heavier tail than the source distribution. We also show that this control of the transferability is needed for any estimator to achieve good rates of convergence. Finally, applying our results to the estimation of treatment effects, we could relax the assumption that the assignment probabilities had to be bounded away from zero and one.

</details>


### [138] [Tests for the mean of high-dimensional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16033)
*Dietmar Ferger*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统计量 V_n = n||X̄_n||² 的高维数据均值检验方法，无需协方差矩阵求逆，适用于维度可任意增长（无显式速率限制）的场景。通过将观测嵌入 l₂ 希尔伯特空间，建立了固定维度和递增维度下的渐近分布理论，并证明了 bootstrap 逼近的渐近有效性。该 bootstrap 检验无需稀疏性假设或协方差矩阵的结构条件即可得到渐近水平 α 的检验程序。核心工具是新的 l₂ 空间中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据中，传统均值检验方法（如 Hotelling T²）因协方差矩阵求逆困难而失效，现有方法多依赖于稀疏性假设或对维度增长有严格速率限制。需要一种无需协方差矩阵求逆、不依赖特定结构假设、且允许维度任意增长的均值检验方法。

Method: (1) 提出统计量 V_n = n||X̄_n||²（样本均值向量的 l₂ 范数平方），避免协方差矩阵求逆；(2) 将观测数据嵌入 l₂ 希尔伯特空间进行分析；(3) 建立新的 l₂ 空间中心极限定理作为理论基础；(4) 证明固定维度和递增维度下的渐近分布结果；(5) 证明 bootstrap 逼近的渐近有效性，构建无需稀疏性或结构假设的 bootstrap 检验程序。

Result: (1) 建立了固定维度和递增维度下 V_n 统计量的渐近分布理论；(2) 证明了 bootstrap 近似的渐近有效性；(3) 得到了渐近水平 α 的 bootstrap 检验程序，且该程序不依赖于稀疏性假设或协方差矩阵的结构条件。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效解决了高维数据均值检验中协方差矩阵求逆的难题，通过 l₂ 空间嵌入和新中心极限定理，实现了无需结构假设的可行检验，为高维统计推断提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We consider the problem of testing the mean of high-dimensional data when the dimension may grow without explicit rate restrictions relative to the sample size. The proposed procedure is based on the statistic V_n = n||Xn||^2, which avoids inversion of the covariance matrix and is therefore suitable for high-dimensional settings.We establish asymptotic distributional results for both fixed and increasing dimension by embedding the observations into the Hilbert space l2. Furthermore, we prove the asymptotic validity of a bootstrap approximation for the distribution of the test statistic. The resulting bootstrap test yields asymptotic level-a procedures without requiring sparsity assumptions or structural conditions on the covariance matrix. In all this, a new Central Limit Theorem in l2 is proving to be an extremely useful tool.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [TeamTR: Trust-Region Fine-Tuning for Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15207)
*Yi Xie,Siao Liu,Falong Fan,Yuanqi Yao,Yue Zhao,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TeamTR框架，解决多智能体LLM系统在顺序微调中因上下文分布偏移导致的性能退化问题。通过信任区域方法和轨迹重采样，实现了每智能体更新后的分布控制，将评估偏差从二次方降至线性，平均提升7.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在复杂推理中虽有潜力，但近期评估显示其常不如单模型基线。作者发现顺序微调共享上下文团队时存在结构性故障：更新一个智能体会改变团队的上下文分布，当后续更新在缓存的旧轨迹上评估时，这种分布不匹配会累积放大。

Method: 形式化定义了复合占用偏移（compounding occupancy shift），证明陈旧占用评估的惩罚随智能体数量呈二次方增长，而中间占用评估可降至线性。提出TeamTR信任区域框架：每次组件更新后重新采样轨迹，并强制每智能体散度控制，推导出严格的每更新和每阶段改进下界。

Result: 实验表明TeamTR平均优于单智能体和顺序微调基线7.1%，缓解了协调退化问题，并支持即插即用的组件替换。

Conclusion: TeamTR通过信任区域方法和轨迹重采样有效解决了多智能体顺序微调中的分布偏移累积问题，为多智能体LLM系统的稳定训练提供了理论保证和实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems have shown promise for complex reasoning, yet recent evaluations reveal they often underperform single-model baselines. We identify a structural failure mode in sequential fine-tuning of shared-context teams: updating one agent shifts the team's context distribution, and when subsequent updates are evaluated on cached rollouts, this mismatch compounds. We formalize this as the compounding occupancy shift and prove that stale-occupancy evaluation incurs a penalty that scales quadratically with the number of agents. In contrast, intermediate-occupancy evaluation reduces this to linear scaling. We propose TeamTR, a trust-region framework that resamples trajectories after each component update and enforces per-agent divergence control, yielding rigorous per-update and per-stage improvement lower bounds. Experiments show that TeamTR outperforms single-agent and sequential baselines with 7.1% on average, mitigates coordination regressions, and supports plug-and-play component replacement. Code is available at https://github.com/Yydc/TeamTR.

</details>


### [140] [Quantization Undoes Alignment: Bias Emergence in Compressed LLMs Across Models and Precision Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15208)
*Plawan Kumar Rath,Rahul Maliakkal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对三个指令微调模型在五种精度水平下的系统研究发现，3-bit量化会导致6-21%原无偏样本出现新刻板行为，模型选择"未知"答案的意愿下降17.4%。标准质量指标（困惑度）几乎无变化，但4-bit时已有2.5-5.6%的样本出现新偏差，表明聚合评估指标系统性遗漏公平性关键退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化被广泛用于压缩大语言模型以降低推理成本和内存占用，但其对模型质量的影响尚未被充分理解。现有研究通常只比较两种条件（全精度与单一量化变体）、依赖聚合偏差指标、且仅评估单一模型族，无法区分渐进退化与阈值依赖的安全失效。

Method: 对三个指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Phi-3.5-mini）在五个精度水平（BF16到3-bit）上进行受控实证研究，使用12,148个BBQ偏差基准样本、5个随机种子，总计911,100条推理记录。

Result: 3-bit量化导致6-21%原无偏样本出现新刻板行为，呈现清晰剂量-反应模式（逻辑回归证实）；模型选择"未知"答案的意愿下降17.4%。标准质量指标几乎无变化：困惑度在8-bit时增长不足0.5%，4-bit时不足3%；但4-bit时已有2.5-5.6%的样本出现新偏差。

Conclusion: 聚合评估指标系统性遗漏公平性关键退化，强调需要质量感知的压缩协议，在部署前显式测试偏差涌现。

Abstract: Large Language Models are routinely compressed via post-training quantization to reduce inference costs and memory footprint for cloud and edge deployment, yet the impact of this compression on model quality remains poorly understood. Existing studies typically compare only two conditions (full-precision vs. a single quantized variant), rely on aggregate bias metrics, and evaluate a single model family, making it impossible to distinguish gradual degradation from threshold-dependent safety failures. We conduct a controlled empirical study of three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Phi-3.5-mini) at five precision levels (BF16 through 3-bit) on 12,148 BBQ bias benchmark items across 5 random seeds, totaling 911,100 inference records. Our results reveal that 3-bit quantization causes 6-21% of previously unbiased items to develop new stereotypical behaviors, following a clear dose-response pattern confirmed via logistic regression, while models' willingness to select "unknown" answers declines by 17.4%. Crucially, these item-level changes are invisible to standard quality metrics: perplexity increases by less than 0.5% at 8-bit and under 3% at 4-bit across all three models, yet 2.5-5.6% of items already develop new biases at 4-bit. These findings demonstrate that aggregate evaluation metrics systematically miss fairness-critical degradation, underscoring the need for quality-aware compression protocols that explicitly test for bias emergence before deployment.

</details>


### [141] [MuteBench: Modality Unavailability Tolerance Evaluation for Incomplete Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15235)
*Wugeng Zheng,Ziwen Kan,Tianlong Chen,Chen Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuteBench是一个多模态生理数据缺失鲁棒性基准，覆盖9个数据集、6种融合架构和2种缺失模式（模态缺失与模态内缺失），发现架构族是鲁棒性最强预测因子，通道独立模型容忍模态缺失但对模态内缺失敏感，课程模态丢失保护效果受训练最大丢失率限制，扩散插补能改善下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生理数据在临床AI系统中传感器经常失效，存在模态缺失和模态内缺失两种常见故障模式，但现有基准无法在控制严重程度下评估多种融合架构对两种故障模式的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建MuteBench基准，覆盖7个临床领域的9个数据集、6种融合架构、2种缺失数据模式，共125,000个样本，系统评估不同架构在受控缺失程度下的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 架构族是鲁棒性最强预测因子，优于参数数量；通道独立模型对模态缺失鲁棒但对模态内缺失敏感；课程模态丢失仅在训练最大丢失率内有效；通道数、序列长度和模态对齐共同决定哪种缺失模式威胁更大；扩散插补在下游分类任务中有效，尤其对专家路由敏感的模型提升最大。

Conclusion: MuteBench为实践者选择现有架构和设计未来鲁棒多模态融合方法提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological data powers clinical AI systems from intensive care units to wearable devices, but sensors routinely fail in practice. Two failure modes are common: modality missing, where an entire channel is absent, and within-modality missing, where a contiguous time segment is lost. No existing benchmark evaluates multiple fusion architectures under both failure modes at controlled severity levels across diverse clinical datasets. We present MuteBench, a benchmark covering 9 datasets from 7 clinical domains, 6 fusion architectures, and 2 missing-data modes over 125,000 samples. Through this benchmark, we find that architecture family is the strongest predictor of robustness, outweighing parameter count. Channel-independent models tolerate modality missing well but can be sensitive to within-modality missing, especially on short sequences. Curriculum modality dropout protects reliably only up to the maximum dropout rate used in training. We also find that channel count, sequence length, and modality alignment jointly determine which failure mode poses the greater threat. Finally, a PTB-XL case study suggests that diffusion-based imputation can improve downstream classification under within-modality missing, with the largest gains for models whose expert routing is most sensitive to corrupted inputs, though broader validation across datasets remains an open direction. MuteBench provides practitioners with concrete guidance for both selecting existing architectures and informing the design of future robust multimodal fusion methods.

</details>


### [142] [Reducing the Safety Tax in LLM Safety Alignment with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15239)
*Yu Fu,Longxuan Yu,Haz Sameen Shahgir,Zhipeng Wei,Hui Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OPSA（在线策略自我蒸馏）方法，通过让模型生成自身轨迹并用冻结的教师副本进行逐token KL散度监督，解决了安全对齐中的策略外训练不匹配问题，在多个推理模型上实现了更好的安全-推理权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐通常以牺牲推理能力为代价（安全税），现有方法主要关注监督微调导致的数据分布不匹配，但忽略了策略外训练不匹配这一来源。

Method: 提出OPSA方法：模型自我生成rollout轨迹，由冻结的教师模型（基于特权安全上下文）提供逐token KL散度监督；引入教师翻转率（teacher flip rate）作为选择有效安全上下文的准则。

Result: 在两个推理模型家族、五个模型规模上，OPSA在安全-推理权衡上优于策略外自我蒸馏和外部教师蒸馏，小模型提升最大（R1-Distill-1.5B +8.85，Qwen3-0.6B +5.49）。

Conclusion: OPSA通过在线策略训练解决了安全对齐中的策略外不匹配问题，且逐token分析表明其更新集中在早期合规决策token上，从而在提升安全性的同时保留推理能力。

Abstract: Safety alignment often improves robustness to harmful queries at the cost of reasoning ability, a tradeoff known as the safety tax. A common cause is distributional mismatch: supervised fine-tuning trains the target model on safety demonstrations produced by humans, external models, or fixed self-generated traces, rather than on trajectories sampled from its own policy. We identify off-policy training mismatch as a second source of this tax and study on-policy self-distillation for safety alignment, which we call OPSA. The model generates its own rollouts and receives dense per-token KL supervision from a frozen teacher copy of itself conditioned on a privileged safety context. Because this teacher must be safer than the sampled student trajectory, we introduce \emph{teacher flip rate}: a criterion that measures how often a privileged context converts unsafe responses into safe ones. We use this signal to search for contexts that activate latent safety reasoning rather than merely elicit safe-looking demonstrations. Across two reasoning-model families and five model scales, OPSA achieves a stronger safety--reasoning tradeoff than off-policy self-distillation and external-teacher distillation under matched data and full-parameter fine-tuning, with the largest gains on smaller models (+8.85 points on R1-Distill-1.5B and +5.49 points on Qwen3-0.6B). The gains persist across training-set sizes and adaptive jailbreak evaluations. Token-level analyses further show that OPSA concentrates updates near early compliance-decision tokens, providing a mechanism for improving safety while preserving general reasoning.

</details>


### [143] [Reading the Cell, Designing the Cure: Perturbation-Conditioned Molecular Diffusion for Function-Oriented Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15243)
*Ziyu Xu,Zijian Zhang,Liang Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出转录组引导的药物设计（TBDD）框架CURE（Cellular Response Engine），通过多分辨率扩散模型将药物分子生成条件化为期望的转录组状态转变，并设计转录组扰动功能特征提取器（TFE）解决跨模态域gap和信号稀疏问题，在标准基准和分布外测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当可靠靶标结构不可得或表型源于失调通路时，转录组扰动可作为药物作用的系统级功能读数。现有方法缺乏从转录组状态直接生成分子的能力，且面临生物学与化学之间的巨大领域差异以及转录组信号的稀疏性问题。

Method: 提出CURE模型：一个多分辨率转录组引导的扩散框架，核心组件为Transcriptome Perturbation Functional Feature Extractor (TFE)，包含三个功能——(1)从扰动前后状态中蒸馏功能导向的扰动嵌入；(2)将这些特征对齐到双化学视图以桥接跨模态差异；(3)执行异质性感知聚合从噪声转录组数据中提取稳健的状态特异性信号。

Result: 在标准基准和严格的分布外测试协议上，CURE在结构质量和功能一致性方面持续优于强基线方法；零样本基因抑制剂设计任务验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 转录组驱动的表型导向生成式发现具有巨大潜力，CURE为从系统级功能读向进行药物设计提供了有效框架。

Abstract: When reliable target structures are unavailable at scale or phenotypes arise from dysregulated pathways, transcriptomic perturbations provide a system-level functional readout for drug action. In this work, we formalize \emph{Transcriptome-based Drug Design (TBDD)} as a generative inverse problem: designing drug molecules conditioned on desired transcriptomic state transitions. We analyze the inherently ill-posed nature of this task, which is further complicated by the profound domain gap between biology and chemistry and by the sparsity of transcriptomic signals. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{\themodel{}} (A \textbf{C}ell\textbf{U}lar \textbf{R}esponse \textbf{E}ngine), a multi-resolution transcriptome-guided diffusion framework. \themodel{} features a specialized \textbf{Transcriptome Perturbation Functional Feature Extractor (TFE)} that (1) distills function-oriented perturbation embeddings from pre/post states, (2) aligns these signatures to dual chemical views to bridge the cross-modal gap, and (3) performs heterogeneity-aware aggregation to extract robust state-specific signals from noisy transcriptomic data. Extensive evaluations on both standard benchmarks and rigorous out-of-distribution protocols demonstrate that \themodel{} consistently outperforms strong baselines in structural quality and functional consistency. Furthermore, we validate its practical utility via a zero-shot gene-inhibitor design task, highlighting the potential of phenotype-driven generative discovery.

</details>


### [144] [Privacy Evaluation of Generative Models for Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15246)
*Stavros Bouras,Ioannis Kontopoulos,Chiara Pugliese,Francesco Lettich,Emanuele Carlini,Hanna Kavalionak,Chiara Renso,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了生成式轨迹模型的隐私保护问题，通过成员推理攻击证明生成式模型并不能天然消除隐私风险，并指出当前隐私评估方法存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹数据对现代城市智能至关重要，但其敏感性引发严重隐私问题。尽管GAN、VAE、扩散模型等生成式模型被用于生成合成轨迹数据，且常被认为因生成性质而能保护隐私，但这一假设并不成立。作者旨在填补生成式轨迹建模与隐私评估之间的交叉领域的研究空白。

Method: 首先梳理了适用于轨迹生成任务的隐私评估实证方法，识别出当前隐私评估中的显著不足；在此基础上，针对代表性生成式轨迹模型实施了成员推理攻击（Membership Inference Attacks），以验证这些模型的隐私风险。

Result: 发现现有生成式轨迹模型的隐私评估存在明显缺口；成员推理攻击的实验结果表明，生成式模型并不能天然消除隐私风险，隐私泄露是切实存在的。

Conclusion: 生成式轨迹模型虽然能生成逼真的合成数据，但其生成性质并不能自动保证隐私安全；需要更严谨的隐私评估方法来衡量和保障轨迹生成任务中的隐私保护水平。

Abstract: Trajectory data is fundamental to modern urban intelligence, yet its sensitivity raises significant privacy concerns. Generative models such as Generative Adversarial Networks, Variational Autoencoders, and Diffusion Models have been developed to generate realistic synthetic trajectory data by capturing underlying spatiotemporal distributions and mobility patterns. Although these models are often assumed to preserve privacy due to their generative nature, this assumption does not necessarily hold. In this work, we investigate the intersection of generative trajectory modeling and privacy evaluation. By identifying applicable empirical methods for assessing privacy preservation in trajectory generation tasks, we demonstrate a significant gap in the evaluation of privacy for generative trajectory models. Motivated by this gap, we implement Membership Inference Attacks against representative models, demonstrating the feasibility of using such empirical privacy evaluation methods and showing that their generative nature does not eliminate privacy risks.

</details>


### [145] [GQLA: Group-Query Latent Attention for Hardware-Adaptive Large Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15250)
*Fanxu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GQLA作为MLA的轻量改进，通过单一权重集暴露MQA吸收和GQA两条等价的解码路径，无需重训练即可在不同硬件上分别达到最优性能（H100上MQA吸收路径、H20上GQA路径），并支持8路零冗余张量并行及MTP加速；通过TransGQLA方法可将预训练GQA模型转换为GQLA，在LLaMA-3-8B上将KV缓存压缩至GQA基线的28.125%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA虽然在DeepSeek-V2/V3中实现了近乎完美的H100 roofline性能，但其训练权重只暴露了单一的MQA吸收解码路径，这种路径与H100级别的算力-带宽比紧密耦合，无法在H20等推理GPU上利用张量并行和MTP增益，限制了其在受限硬件上的部署效率。

Method: GQLA对MLA进行最小化修改，使训练后的单一权重集同时支持两条代数等价的解码路径：①MQA吸收路径（与MLA相同，适用于H100）；②GQA路径（每组扩展缓存，适用于H20）。运行时根据目标硬件自动选择最优路径，无需重训练或自定义内核。同时提出TransGQLA方法，可将预训练的GQA模型转换为GQLA模型。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上，TransGQLA在MQA吸收路径上将每token KV缓存压缩至GQA基线的28.125%，同时在分组路径上结构性地保持GQA级别的通信量；GQLA在H100上通过MQA吸收路径达到roofline上限，在H20上通过GQA+MTP路径（s_q=2）达到roofline上限，并支持最多8路零冗余张量并行。

Conclusion: GQLA通过单一权重集适配不同硬件特性，解决了MLA在受限GPU上的部署效率问题，兼容性好且无需重训练，为多头潜在注意力机制的硬件自适应部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA), the attention used in DeepSeek-V2/V3, jointly compresses keys and values into a low-rank latent and matches the H100 roofline almost perfectly. Its trained weights, however, expose only one decoding path - an absorbed MQA form - which ties efficient inference to H100-class compute-bandwidth ratios, forfeits tensor parallelism along the head axis, and yields no Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) gain on commodity inference GPUs such as the export-restricted H20. We propose Group-Query Latent Attention (GQLA), a minimal modification of MLA whose trained weights expose two algebraically equivalent decoding paths over the same parameters: an MQA-absorb path identical to MLA's, and a GQA path with a per-group expanded cache. The runtime picks the path that matches the target hardware - no retraining, no custom kernels - so a single set of GQLA weights pins the rooflines of both H100 (MQA-absorb, s_q=1) and H20 (GQA + MTP, s_q=2), while supporting up to 8-way zero-redundancy tensor parallelism on the GQA path. To avoid pretraining from scratch we extend TransMLA into TransGQLA, which converts a pretrained GQA checkpoint into a GQLA model; on LLaMA-3-8B it compresses the per-token KV cache to 28.125% of the GQA baseline on the MQA-absorb path while structurally preserving GQA-level traffic on the per-group path.

</details>


### [146] [PDRNN: Modular Data-driven Pedestrian Dead Reckoning on Loosely Coupled Radio- and Inertial-Signalstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15252)
*Peter Bauer,Andreas Porada,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG
