TL;DR: 提出PDRNN，一种模块化混合AI辅助行人航位推算系统，利用RNN隐式预测异步传感器数据流，通过独立ML模型分别估计方向、速度/距离和位置，并融合不确定度信息，在动态运动数据上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDR系统在融合多模态传感器数据时面临采样率不一致、传输不可靠的问题，且在高加速度、高速度、快速方向变化的动态运动中难以有效融合数据，导致误差积累。

Method: 提出PDRNN系统：1) 采用简单RNN架构隐式预测来自不同估计方法的异步传感器数据流；2) 模块化设计，每个组件作为独立ML模型集成，分别估计方向、速度/距离、位置等关键参数及其方差；3) 利用加速度和陀螺仪数据通过独立ML模型估计方向、速度和距离，可选5G等同步无线电系统提供绝对位置进行稳定；4) 最终融合模型结合位置、速度和方向输出，利用不确定度估计增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在动态运动数据上的实验表明，PDRNN在精度和准确度上优于经典方法和基于ML的方法，有效避免了黑盒方法中常见的误差积累问题。

Conclusion: PDRNN尽管增加了系统复杂度，但提供了预测能力和更好的组件控制能力，是一种有效的模块化混合AI辅助PDR解决方案。

Abstract: Modern pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR) systems rely on fusing noisy and biased estimates of position, velocity, and calibrated orientation derived from loosely coupled sensors to determine the current pose of a localized object. However, discrepancies in the sampling rates of sensor-specific estimation methods and unreliable transmission pose significant challenges. And traditional methods often fail to effectively fuse multimodal sensor data during dynamic movements characterized by high accelerations, velocities, and rapidly varying orientations. To address these limitations, we propose a simple recurrent neural network (RNN) architecture capable of implicitly forecasting asynchronous sensor data streams from diverse estimation methods along reference trajectories. The proposed approach introduces PDRNN, a modular hybrid AI-assisted PDR system that handles each component as an independent ensemble of machine learning (ML) models to estimate both key parameter means and variances. Separate ML-based models are employed to estimate orientation, (un)directed velocity or distance from acceleration and gyroscope data, with optional absolute positioning from synchronized radio systems such as 5G for stabilization. A final fusion model combines these outputs, position, velocity, and orientation, while using uncertainty estimates to enhance system robustness. The modular design allows individual components to be updated, fine-tuned, or replaced without affecting the entire system. Experiments on dynamic sports movement data show that PDRNN achieves superior accuracy and precision compared to classic and ML-based methods, effectively avoiding error accumulation common in black-box approaches. And PDRNN offers forecast capabilities and better component control despite increased system complexity.

</details>


### [147] [Position: Ideas Should be the Center of Machine Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15253)
*Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"思想优先"（Ideas First）框架，主张机器学习研究应聚焦于思想本身及其在模型中预测的行为特征，而非过度沉迷于基准测试或纯理论，从而弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟，并降低研究门槛促进公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习研究分裂为两种互不连通的主流模式：一种是以基准测试为导向的工程化研究，追求指标优化而忽视对模型本质的理解；另一种是理想化的理论研究，往往无法应用到现代系统中。两种模式都过度关注最终结果（基准分数或理论定理），而忽略了核心科学对象——思想本身。

Method: 提出"思想优先"（Ideas First）框架，核心观点是：思想的价值应体现在它们在现代模型中预测的行为特征（behavioral signatures）上，并通过设计有针对性的实验来检测这些模式，而非为了冲击排行榜。该框架将基准测试和定理视为检验机制性假设的工具，而非研究目标本身。

Result: 该框架能够在理论与实践之间搭建桥梁，同时通过消除"复杂性溢价"（complexity premium）促进研究公平，使资源有限的研究者也能做出严谨的科学贡献。

Conclusion: 倡导建立以思想为中心的研究文化，将基准测试和定理视为检验假设的手段而非目的，从而推动更具科学性和包容性的机器学习研究生态。

Abstract: Machine learning research increasingly bifurcates into two disconnected modes: benchmark-driven engineering that prioritizes metrics over understanding, and idealized theory that often fails to transfer to modern systems. In this position paper, we argue that the field focuses too heavily on these endpoints, neglecting the central scientific object: the idea. We propose an Ideas First framework in which ideas are valued for the behavioral signatures they predict in modern models, and these signatures are tested through tailored experiments designed to detect the relevant patterns rather than to win leaderboards. This shift not only bridges the gap between theory and practice but also promotes equity by removing the "complexity premium," enabling rigorous scientific contributions from researchers with modest computational, financial, and human resources. Ultimately, we advocate for a research culture centered on ideas, treating benchmarks and theorems as instruments for testing mechanistic hypotheses rather than as ends in themselves.

</details>


### [148] [Training on Documents About Monitoring Leads to CoT Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15257)
*Reilly Haskins,Bilal Chughtai,Joshua Engels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在了解存在思维链（CoT）监控的情况下，模型是否会通过隐藏其真实推理过程来逃避检测。通过合成文档微调暴露模型于CoT监控知识，发现知晓监控的模型比不知情的模型更成功地隐藏其不良行为，且这一效应在更难的任务中仍然存在。CoT可控性与隐藏行为成功率高度相关（r=0.800, p=0.017），强化学习下知晓监控的模型也更快学会在触发监控前获取奖励。结果表明，监控知识结合高CoT可控性对CoT监控方法构成风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是检测模型不当行为的重要手段，但其有效性依赖于模型忠实外化其推理过程。研究者关注这一脆弱性，探究知晓监控存在的模型是否能够隐藏其推理以逃避检测。

Method: 使用合成文档微调方法，将描述CoT监控的真实预训练风格文档暴露给八个模型；比较知晓监控的模型与不知情对照组在隐藏不良行为上的表现差异；分析CoT可控性与隐藏成功率的相关性；研究强化学习条件下两组模型的行为差异。

Result: 知晓监控的模型比不知情对照组持续获得更高的未检测到不良行为率；该效应在更难的任务中仍然存在但减弱；CoT可控性与隐藏成功率显著相关（r=0.800, p=0.017）；强化学习下知晓监控的模型更快学会在不触发CoT监控的情况下获取奖励。

Conclusion: 监控知识结合高CoT可控性对基于CoT的监控方法构成风险，模型可能利用对监控机制的了解来隐藏其真实意图。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the most promising tools we have for detecting model misbehavior, but its effectiveness depends on models faithfully externalizing their reasoning. Motivated by this vulnerability, we study whether monitor-aware models are capable of obfuscating their reasoning to evade detection. We use synthetic document finetuning to expose eight models to realistic pre-training-style documents describing a CoT monitor and find that monitor-aware models consistently achieve higher rates of undetected misbehavior compared to unaware controls. This effect is weaker but still present on a harder agentic task. We also show that CoT controllability, a model's ability to reshape its own reasoning trace under an imposed constraint, is closely correlated with obfuscation success across the eight models studied ($r=0.800$, $p=0.017$). Monitor-aware models placed under equal reinforcement learning optimization pressure also learn to reward-hack without triggering a CoT monitor substantially faster than unaware controls. Together, these results suggest that knowledge of monitoring combined with high CoT controllability poses a risk to CoT-based monitoring.

</details>


### [149] [Tadpole: Autoencoders as Foundation Models for 3D PDEs with Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15284)
*Qiang Liu,Felix Koehler,Benjamin Holzschuh,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tadpole是一个针对三维偏微分方程的基础模型，通过在线数据生成框架预训练为自编码器，具备跨物理系统的可迁移性、高维扩展性和多功能性，在下游任务（动力学学习、生成建模）中通过参数高效微调表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D PDE模型在可迁移性、高维扩展性和多任务泛化方面存在局限，缺乏一个能够统一处理不同物理系统的基础模型。

Method: 采用在线数据生成框架高效生成合成3D PDE训练数据，预训练单通道空间块的自编码器；针对动力学学习提出参数高效微调策略，融合低秩适应、潜在空间变换和重新引入的跳跃连接。

Result: 预训练扩展到等效数百TB数据量；微调后在多种下游任务（动力学学习、生成建模）上表现优异，仅需极少可训练参数即可实现准确的时序建模。

Conclusion: Tadpole验证了自编码器预训练策略在3D PDE基础模型中的有效性，展示了作为通用基础模型在多种物理系统中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Tadpole, a novel foundation model for three-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) that addresses key challenges in transferability, scalability to high dimensionality, and multi-functionality. Tadpole is pre-trained as an autoencoder on synthetic 3D PDE data generated by an efficient online data-generation framework. This enables large-scale, diverse training without storage or I/O overhead, demonstrated by scaling to an equivalent of hundreds of terabytes of training data. By autoencoding single-channel spatial crops, Tadpole learns rich and transferable representations across heterogeneous physical systems with varying numbers of state variables and spatial resolutions. Although pre-trained solely as an autoencoder, Tadpole can be efficiently applied for multiple downstream tasks beyond reconstruction, including dynamics learning and generative modeling. For dynamics learning, we propose a novel parameter-efficient fine-tuning strategy that integrates low-rank adaptation, latent-space transformations, and reintroduced skip connections, achieving accurate temporal modeling with a minimal number of trainable parameters. Tadpole demonstrates strong fine-tuning performance across various downstream tasks, highlighting its versatility and effectiveness as a foundation model for 3D PDE learning. Source code and pre-trained weights of Tadpole are available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/tadpole

</details>


### [150] [Universal Approximation of Nonlinear Operators and Their Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15285)
*Filippo de Feo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了Banach空间之间非线性k次可微算子及其导数的通用逼近定理（UATs），将Hornik (1991)的经典结果完整推广到无限维设置和算子学习领域。该工作基于编码器-解码器架构，利用Bastiani意义下的连续可微性、紧致-开拓扑和加权Sobolev空间等关键技术，为算子学习中的导数信息学习（DIOL）奠定了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子学习（OL）领域虽已取得显著进展，但关于非线性算子及其导数的通用逼近定理（UATs）仍是一个开放的基础性问题。导数信息算子学习（DIOL）作为算子学习的前沿方向，其理论基础的缺失制约了高精度算子学习、Banach空间中的快速约束优化（如PDE最优控制、反问题）以及无限维PDE数值方法（如HJB方程、SPDE等）的发展。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构（涵盖DeepONets、Deep-H-ONets、PCA-Nets等经典结构）对非线性算子进行参数化。基于四个关键技术要素：(i) Banach空间的逼近性质；(ii) Bastiani意义下的k次连续可微性（弱于Fréchet可微性）；(iii) 紧致-开拓扑（证明标准算子范数拓扑下即使Fréchet导数也无法实现通用逼近）；(iv) 构造新型加权Sobolev空间用于通用逼近分析。

Result: 首次证明了Banach空间之间非线性k次可微算子及其导数的通用逼近定理，在紧集上一致收敛和在加权Sobolev范数下对一般有限输入测度均成立。这是对Hornik (1991)经典结果在无限维设置和算子学习中的完整推广。

Conclusion: 本文建立的DIOL理论基础可直接应用于：(1) 高阶精度算子学习；(2) Banach空间中快速约束优化（如PDE最优控制、反问题）；(3) 无限维PDE数值方法（如来自PDE最优控制的Banach空间上的HJB方程、SPDE、路径依赖系统、部分观测系统、平均场控制等）。

Abstract: Derivative-Informed Operator Learning (DIOL), i.e. learning a (nonlinear) operator and its derivatives, is an open research frontier at the foundations of the influential field of Operator Learning (OL). In particular, Universal Approximation Theorems (UATs) of nonlinear operators and their derivatives are foundational open questions and delicate problems in nonlinear functional analysis. In this manuscript, we prove the first UATs of non-linear $k$-times differentiable operators between Banach spaces and their derivatives, uniformly on compact sets and in weighted Sobolev norms for general finite input measures, via OL architectures. Our results are the first complete generalizations of the corresponding influential classical results in [Hornik, 1991] to infinite-dimensional settings and OL.
  We discuss several open areas where DIOL and our UATs find applications: high-order accuracy in OL, fast constrained optimization in Banach spaces (e.g. optimal control of PDEs, inverse problems) and numerical methods for infinite-dimensional PDEs (e.g. HJB PDEs on Banach spaces from optimal control of PDEs, SPDEs, path-dependent systems, partially observed systems, mean-field control).
  We parameterize nonlinear operators via Encoder-Decoder Architectures, renowned classes in OL due to their generality, including classical architectures, such as DeepONets, Deep-H-ONets, PCA-Nets. Our results are based on four key features that allow us to prove UATs in full generality: (i) Approximation Properties of Banach spaces. (ii) $k$-times continuous differentiability in the sense of Bastiani (weaker than $k$-times continuous Fréchet differentiability). (iii) Natural compact-open topologies for UA; indeed, we show that UA in standard compact-open topologies induced by operator norms is violated even for Fréchet derivatives. (iv) Construction of novel weighted Sobolev spaces for the UA.

</details>


### [151] [GQA-μP: The maximal parameterization update for grouped query attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15290)
*Kyle R. Chickering,Huijuan Wang,Mengxi Wu,Alexander Moreno,Muhao Chen,Xuezhe Ma,Daria Soboleva,Joel Hestness,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于谱特征学习视角，将权重谱范数条件从启发式提升为特征学习的定义，从而推导出Complete-P深度和权重衰减缩放，并通过改进谱范数处理非满秩情况，首次推导出分组查询注意力（GQA）的μP缩放，实验验证了学习率和权重衰减的超参数迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少大规模语言模型调优所需的计算量，解决μP（最大更新参数化）方法对新架构推导困难的问题。

Method: 1. 将谱范数条件从启发式提升为特征学习的定义，推导出Complete-P深度和权重衰减缩放；2. 提出改进的谱范数以处理非满秩权重矩阵，从而推导GQA的μP缩放。

Result: 首次推导出GQA架构的μP缩放规则，实验验证了学习率在GQA重复超参数上的迁移以及权重衰减的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 谱范数视角为特征学习提供了更严格的理论基础，并成功将μP扩展到更广泛的架构（如GQA），有效减少了LLM调优的计算成本。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer across model architectures dramatically reduces the amount of compute necessary for tuning large language models (LLMs). The maximal update parameterization (μP) ensures transfer through principled mathematical analysis but can be challenging to derive for new model architectures. Building on the spectral feature-learning view of Yang et al. (2023a), we make two advances. First, we promote spectral norm conditions on the weights from a heuristic to the definition of feature learning, and as a consequence arrive at the Complete-P depth and weight-decay scalings without recourse to lazy-learning. Second, we consider a modified spectral norm that preserves the valid scaling law of network weights when weight matrices are not full rank. This enables (to our knowledge, the first) derivation of μP scalings for grouped-query attention (GQA). We demonstrate the efficacy of our theoretical derivations by showing learning rate transfer across the GQA repetition hyperparameter as well as experiments regarding transfer over weight decay.

</details>


### [152] [GESD: Beyond Outcome-Oriented Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15295)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向过程的公平性指标GESD，用于衡量模型解释在不同子群间的稳定性差异，并将其整合到多目标优化框架FEU中，同时优化效用、结果公平和解释公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性指标（如统计均等、均等机会）仅关注结果层面的差异，无法反映决策过程或解释中存在的偏见，缺乏对模型解释稳定性、鲁棒性和敏感性的公平性评估。

Method: 提出GESD（组级解释稳定性差异）指标，衡量不同保护子群间模型解释的稳定性、鲁棒性和敏感性差异；进一步将其整合到FEU（公平-解释-效用）多目标优化框架中，联合优化效用、结果公平和解释公平。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，GESD有效捕捉了群组间的解释质量差异；FEU在效用和公平性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GESD通过连接结果公平和解释公平，为诊断和缓解预测模型中的偏见提供了更全面的工具。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) algorithms are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making domains such as loan approvals, hiring, and recidivism predictions. While existing fairness metrics (e.g., statistical parity, equal opportunity) effectively quantify outcome-oriented disparities, they offer limited insight into the procedure or explanation behind biased decisions. To address this gap, we propose Group-level Explanation Stability Disparity (GESD), a \textit{procedural-oriented} fairness metric that measures disparities in the stability, robustness, and sensitivity of model explanations across different subgroups in a protected category. %GESD is explainer-agnostic, model-agnostic, and extends the scope of fairness analyses to the level of explainability. We further integrate GESD into a multi-objective optimization framework that jointly optimizes for utility, outcome-based fairness, and explanation-based fairness called FEU (Fairness--Explainability--Utility). Empirical results on multiple benchmark datasets show that GESD effectively captures group-wise discrepancies in explanation quality, and that FEU improves both utility and fairness over state-of-the-art methods. By bridging outcome-based and explanation-based fairness, GESD offers a comprehensive tool for diagnosing and mitigating bias in predictive modeling. Our code and datasets are available on GitHub {\hyperlink{https://github.com/horlahsunbo/GESD}{https://github.com/horlahsunbo/GESD}}

</details>


### [153] [From I/O to Code with Discovery Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15334)
*Yihong Dong,Jiaru Qian,Haoran Zhang,Peixu Wang,Binhua Li,Zhi Jin,Yongbin Li,Ge Li,Xiaokang Yang,Xue Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIO-Agent，一种基于LLM的发现式智能体，通过进化搜索和变换优先级前提（TPP）偏置，从输入-输出示例中自动综合程序（IO2Code），在多个难度级别上优于传统方法和现有进化智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NL2Code已取得巨大成功，但从输入-输出行为综合程序（IO2Code）这一更具挑战性的任务仍基本未解决。IO2Code需要从具体计算行为中恢复底层原理，面临巨大且欠约束的假设空间，而LLM在预训练中缺乏自然语言与代码之间的语义对齐先验。

Method: 将IO2Code建模为离散程序空间上的进化搜索，LLM作为变异算子，执行错误信号引导变异。引入变换优先级前提（TPP）作为变异先验，偏置LLM优先选择与当前证据一致的最简假设，从常量逐步升级到条件、迭代，仅在简单构造不足时升级。

Result: 构建IO2CodeBench多难度基准。实验表明DIO-Agent在所有难度级别和多种LLM上一致优于传统程序综合方法和SOTA进化智能体基线，并显著超越使用同等采样预算的测试时扩展策略。

Conclusion: DIO-Agent通过进化搜索与TPP先验的结合，有效解决了IO2Code任务中假设空间过大和搜索易陷入复杂死胡同的问题，为从输入-输出示例自动综合程序提供了有效框架。

Abstract: The automatic synthesis of a program from any form of specification is regarded as a holy grail of computer science. Fueled by LLMs, NL2Code has achieved tremendous success, yet the fundamentally more challenging task of synthesizing programs from input-output behavior, which we refer to as IO2Code, remains largely unsolved. Whereas NL2Code can exploit the semantic alignment between natural language and code acquired during pretraining, IO2Code requires recovering underlying principles from concrete computational behavior, navigating a vast and underspecified hypothesis space. To address this, we propose DIO-Agent, a discovery agent for IO2Code. Our method frames IO2Code as an evolutionary search over discrete program space, in which an LLM serves as the mutation operator and concrete error signals from execution guide each mutation. To prevent the search from wandering into structurally complex yet incorrect dead ends, we introduce the Transformation Priority Premise as a mutation prior that biases the LLM toward the simplest hypothesis consistent with current evidence, progressively escalating from constants to conditionals to iteration only when simpler constructs are insufficient. To facilitate systematic study, we further construct an IO2CodeBench spanning multiple difficulty levels. Extensive experiments show that DIO-Agent consistently outperforms both traditional program-by-example method and SOTA evolution-agent baselines across all difficulty levels and various LLMs, while substantially surpassing test-time scaling strategies with equivalent sampling budgets.

</details>


### [154] [Bounded-Rationality, Hedging, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15340)
*Pedro A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将学习问题建模为有界理性决策问题，其中学习器的响应律决定了训练样本对输出影响的强度及其对冲失真的能力。通过f-散度正则化，推导出训练损失与样本依赖之间的下界权衡曲线和上界认证曲线，并提出从黑盒行为中恢复对冲量以进行泛化认证的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解学习器如何控制训练样本对其输出的影响强度以及抵御失真的能力，并将泛化视为学习器自身响应律的可检验对冲性质。

Method: 将学习问题建模为有界理性决策问题，以从样本到输出的诱导信道为原始对象。用f-散度正则化表示响应律，在信息几何框架下推导权衡曲线和认证曲线，并通过缩放损失和局部损失扰动从黑盒行为中恢复对冲量。

Result: 建立了训练损失与样本依赖之间的下界权衡曲线和上界认证曲线；证明了KL散度对应香农互信息的特例；展示了如何从黑盒行为中恢复对冲量及两条曲线；指出当恢复的对冲量覆盖了特定训练样本引起的失真时，可给出实用的泛化认证。

Conclusion: 泛化被重新定义为学习器自身响应律的可检验对冲性质，而非传统依赖样本外误差的统计性质，提供了基于对冲量的实用泛化认证方法。

Abstract: A learner does not only fit data; it also determines how strongly the training sample may shape its output and how much distortion it can hedge. We study this relation as a bounded-rational decision problem whose primitive object is the induced channel from samples to outputs. The learner's response law determines which changes in this channel are cheap or costly, and therefore induces both a lower tradeoff curve between training loss and sample dependence and a matched upper certificate curve. When the response law is represented by an $f$-divergence regularizer, these curves live in the regularizer's native information geometry, with KL as the special case corresponding to Shannon mutual information. We show how the hedge and the two curves can be recovered from black-box behavior by observing responses to scaled losses and local loss perturbations. In learning, population loss is empirical loss plus the distortion induced by the particular training sample. The recovered hedge gives a practical certificate when it covers that distortion. Thus generalization is treated as a testable hedging property of the learner's own response law.

</details>


### [155] [Representation Without Reward: A JEPA Audit for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15394)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究在自回归语言模型微调中，联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）原则能否通过改善隐藏状态几何结构提升解码任务指标。实验在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA框架下，以自然语言转正则表达式生成任务为基准，比较了22种训练时辅助损失，发现虽有若干辅助项在单检验下显著，但均未通过多重假设检验校正；解码器可见的JEPA目标实现了首个正向辅助-交叉熵梯度余弦，但准确率仍在种子噪声范围内。LoRA与全量微调复制实验均得零结果，表明隐藏状态表征与解码任务准确率在此场景下耦合微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA主张模型应通过预测隐层表征而非观测输出来学习更有用的抽象。对于自回归语言模型微调，该原则意味着诱导出的隐藏状态几何结构必须能够传递到语言模型头并提升解码任务指标。作者旨在严格检验这一要求是否成立。

Method: 在固定Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA框架下，以自然语言转正则表达式生成为任务，比较22种训练时辅助损失，包括轨迹形状正则化、分布约束、预测器/目标非对称性、Fisher度量Jacobi残差，以及一种解码器可见的JEPA目标（构造为位于交叉熵正锥中）。并使用Bonferroni和Holm-Bonferroni进行多重假设检验校正，最后在LoRA和全量微调下进行复制实验。

Result: 若干辅助项在单检验α=0.10下显著（T3-Local最强，Δ=+2.53pp, p=0.003），但无一通过Bonferroni或Holm-Bonferroni校正。解码器可见JEPA首次实现辅助-交叉熵梯度正余弦，但准确率仍在种子噪声范围内。全量微调复制实验确认零结果（TURK: Δ=+0.04pp, p=0.96; SYNTH: Δ=+0.52pp, p=0.28）。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态表征工作与解码任务准确率在此场景下弱耦合，应将LLM领域的JEPA评估重新框架化为耦合问题，核心在于何种度量下有用的隐藏几何结构能成为解码器可见的任务信号。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) propose that a model should learn more useful abstractions when trained to predict latent representations rather than observed outputs. For autoregressive language-model fine-tuning the principle entails a stricter requirement: the induced hidden-state geometry must reach the language-model head \emph{and} improve the decoded task metric. We test that requirement under a fixed Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA harness on natural-language-to-regex generation, comparing twenty-two training-time auxiliaries across trajectory-shape regularisation, distributional constraints, predictor/target asymmetry, Fisher-metric Jacobi residuals, and a decoder-visible JEPA objective constructed to lie in cross-entropy's positive cone. The empirical answer is a structured null: several auxiliaries clear single-cell paired $α= 0.10$ without correction (T3-Local at $Δ= +2.53$~pp, $p = 0.003$ being the strongest), but none survives Bonferroni or Holm--Bonferroni at the relevant family-wise threshold, even though many change curvature, anisotropy, variance, and gradient direction. Decoder-visible JEPA yields the first positive auxiliary--cross-entropy gradient cosine in the study, yet exact match remains inside seed noise; a full-fine-tuning replication of the same auxiliary at $n = 5$ seeds reproduces the null on both benchmarks (TURK: $Δ= +0.04$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.96$; SYNTH: $Δ= +0.52$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.28$), so the null is robust across LoRA and full fine-tuning for the decoder-visible construction. Hidden-state representation work and decoded-task accuracy are therefore weakly coupled in this regime; we accordingly reframe LLM-domain JEPA evaluation as a coupling problem, in which the operative question is under which metrics useful hidden geometry becomes decoder-visible task signal.

</details>


### [156] [LEAP: Trajectory-Level Evaluation of LLMs in Iterative Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15341)
*Marilyn Zhang,Tianfeng Chen,Fabián Barzuna,Ankita Rathod,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAPBench基准框架，通过轨迹面积（AUC）指标、贝叶斯优化基线和文献审计，系统评估LLM在迭代科学设计中的学习效率。发现：最终结果导向的评估在53%任务上改变最佳模型选择；LLM未能超越经典贝叶斯优化基线；领域感知提示反而比领域无关提示更少匹配已发表最优设计；离线强化学习在14/21任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代科学设计基准仅评估固定轮次后的最终结果快照，忽略了反映学习效率的轨迹信息。由于每节省一次迭代都意味着真实的成本和时间节约，需要一种能捕捉学习轨迹的评估方法。

Method: 提出LEAPBench框架，包含55个任务。引入"迄今为止最佳曲线下面积（AUC）"轨迹指标，搭配经典贝叶斯优化作为参考基线，并基于已发表文献进行审计。在8个当代LLM上进行实验，并探索将轨迹指标作为离线强化学习训练目标。

Result: 从最终结果评分切换到轨迹评分，在53%任务上改变了最佳模型的选择。LLM未能超越贝叶斯优化基线。在16个生物学任务中，领域感知提示比领域无关提示匹配已发表最优设计的频率低约10个百分点。在6个文献典型配置与最优配置分歧的任务上，领域无关提示在所有6个任务上都更优。轨迹指标作为奖励进行离线强化学习，在21个保留任务中的14个上提升了性能。

Conclusion: 评估选择（测什么、对比什么基线、以什么为基准）会显著改变关于LLM学习效率的结论。轨迹指标优于最终结果指标，且可作为有效的训练目标。当前LLM在迭代科学设计中的学习效率仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in autonomous laboratories, under the assumption that their domain priors and reasoning over iterative feedback let them converge on good designs in fewer iterations than feedback-only baselines. Current iterative scientific design benchmarks, however, score only outcome snapshots at fixed horizons. This leaves the learning trajectory unmeasured, even though the trajectory is what captures learning efficiency, where each iteration saved is a real saving in cost and time. Motivated by this, we examine three evaluation choices that change the conclusions one draws about LLM learning efficiency in iterative scientific design: what to measure, what baseline to compare against, and what to ground against. We introduce LEAPBench, Learning Efficiency in Adaptive Processes, a 55-task framework that pairs a best-so-far area under the curve (AUC) trajectory metric with a classical Bayesian-optimization reference and an audit grounded in published literature. Applied to eight contemporary LLMs, switching from final-outcome to trajectory scoring changes the best-model decision on 53% of tasks at matched horizons, and exposes efficiency gains overlooked by outcome-based scoring. LLMs do not outperform a classical Bayesian baseline. On 16 biology tasks where the oracle's reward signal is aligned with configurations from the published-best design, domain-aware prompting leads to LLM choices that match the published-best's approximately 10 percentage points less often than domain-agnostic prompting at iteration 30. The pattern is sharpest on 6 tasks where the literature-typical and published-best configurations diverge, and domain-agnostic prompting matches the published-best more often on all 6. The trajectory metric also doubles as a tractable training target. Offline reinforcement learning with the metric as a reward improves performance on 14 of 21 held-out tasks.

</details>


### [157] [$φ$-Balancing for Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15403)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Qi Wang,Runlong Liao,Shuozhe Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出φ-balancing框架，通过凸优化和镜像下降实现专家负载均衡，在预训练和微调中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE负载均衡方法依赖启发式的小批量分配统计，存在相对于总体目标的偏差，需要更精确的均衡方法。

Method: 通过最小化期望路由分布的严格凸、对称、可微势函数，利用凸对偶性导出min-max形式，通过镜像下降实现在线EMA路由调整。

Result: 在大规模预训练和下游微调中，φ-balancing一致优于Switch-style和无损失基线，专家利用更稳定有效。

Conclusion: φ-balancing是一种原则性的、高效的负载均衡框架，能以极小开销显著提升MoE模型的专家利用率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models rely on balanced expert utilization to fully realize their scalability. However, existing load-balancing methods are largely heuristic and operate on noisy mini-batch assignment statistics, introducing bias relative to population-level objectives. We propose $φ$-balancing, a principled framework that directly targets population-level expert balance by minimizing a strictly convex, symmetric, and differentiable potential of the expected routing distribution. Using convex duality, we derive an equivalent min-max formulation and obtain a simple online algorithm via mirror descent, yielding an efficient EMA-based routing adjustment with negligible overhead. Across large-scale pretraining and downstream fine-tuning, $φ$-balancing consistently outperforms prior Switch-style and loss-free baselines, demonstrating more stable and effective expert utilization.

</details>


### [158] [Controllable Molecular Generative Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15354)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Weijiang Li,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMole是一个基于基序感知图扩散的统一分子生成基础模型，通过强化学习优化条件反向策略，在材料与药物发现的九个可控性目标上均排名第一，MAE最高降低48.2%，有效性保持在0.94以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在语言和视觉领域取得了成功，但分子图生成仍缺乏统一的、具有可靠可控性的异构设计任务框架；强化学习虽可作为任务特定优化的后训练机制，但应用于图生成模型时面临原子级动作空间巨大和化学无效中间状态的瓶颈。

Method: 构建统一的基序感知图扩散管道（CoMole），学习基序感知的图空间以将预训练的结构先验迁移到可控生成中；采用强化学习优化化学上有意义的条件反向策略；理论分析了原子级强化学习的瓶颈并证明了基序感知策略优化的合理性。

Result: 在涵盖材料和药物发现的三个异构基准测试中，CoMole在所有九个可控性目标上均排名第一，MAE相比最强基线降低最高48.2%，有效性保持在0.94以上且无需基于规则的校正或后处理过滤；在冻结生成器仅优化任务嵌入时，CoMole的可控性能迁移到未见属性上，性能与强任务特定基线相当。

Conclusion: 基序感知的图扩散与强化学习结合是解决分子图生成中可控性与统一框架问题的有效途径，兼具优异的可控性能、化学有效性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, molecular graph generation still lacks a unified framework for heterogeneous design tasks with reliable controllability. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural post-training mechanism for task-specific optimization, applying it to graph generative models is hindered by the vast atom-wise action spaces and chemically invalid intermediate states. We propose \textbf{Co}ntrollable \textbf{Mole}cular Generative Foundation Models (CoMole), built with a unified motif-aware graph diffusion pipeline. By learning a motif-aware graph space, CoMole transfers pretrained structural priors into controllable generation, where RL optimizes conditional reverse policies over chemically meaningful decisions. We theoretically characterize the bottleneck of atom-level RL and justify motif-aware policy optimization. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks spanning materials and drug discovery, CoMole ranks first in controllability on all nine targets, reduces MAE by up to 48.2% relative to the strongest baselines, and maintains validity above 0.94 without rule-based correction or post-hoc filtering. We further show that CoMole transfers controllability to unseen properties by optimizing only task embeddings with the generator frozen, achieving performance competitive with strong task-specific baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Tighter Regret Bounds for Contextual Action-Set Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15692)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定奖励和转移函数但回合依赖动作集的回合制强化学习，证明了MVP算法可自然地扩展到该框架，并给出了针对对抗性上下文和随机上下文的极小极大遗憾界，以及基于间隙的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实强化学习应用中，智能体常面临每回合可用的动作集动态变化的情况（如推荐系统中的候选集变化），但现有理论工作大多假设固定动作集。本文旨在建立在这种"动作上下文"变化下的强化学习理论框架，并提供最优遗憾保证。

Method: 将MVP算法扩展到回合依赖动作集设定下，分别分析对抗性上下文（上下文由对手选择）和随机上下文（上下文从固定分布中采样）两种场景，并引入全局p-裁剪正间隙的概念推导基于间隙的遗憾界。

Result: 对于对抗性上下文，得到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$的极小极大遗憾界；对于随机上下文，得到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$的遗憾界，进而推出$\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/\varepsilon^2)$的样本复杂度界；基于间隙的遗憾界可在相关次优间隙较大时显著优于极小极大率。

Conclusion: MVP算法可有效处理回合依赖动作集的强化学习问题，其理论保证在不同上下文类型下均接近最优，且基于间隙的分析进一步展示了算法在有利问题实例上的性能优势。

Abstract: We study episodic reinforcement learning with fixed reward and transition functions, but with episode-dependent admissible action sets that are observed at the start of each episode. Performance is measured by cumulative regret against the episode-wise optimal value, $\sum_{k=1}^K [V^{*,M^k} - V^{π^k,M^k}]$, where $M^k$ represents the action context in the $k$-th episode. We show that the MVP algorithm naturally extends to this framework and enjoys strong theoretical guarantees. In particular, we establish a minimax regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ for adversarial contexts, where $L$ denotes the number of possible contexts. This result implies a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ for stochastic contexts. We further translate the stochastic regret guarantee into a sample complexity bound of $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$ for a fixed context distribution.
  In addition, we derive a gap-dependent regret bound of \[ \widetilde O\left( \inf_{p\in [0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{Δ_{\min}^{p}} + pKΔ_{\min}^{p} \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right), \] where $Δ_{\min}^{p}$ is the global $p$-trimmed positive-gap floor over suboptimal $(h,s,a)$ triples. This bound can substantially improve upon the minimax rate when the relevant suboptimality gaps are large.

</details>


### [160] [Is One Score Enough? Rethinking the Evaluation of Sequentially Evolving LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15384)
*Songwei Dong,Zihan Chen,Chengshuai Shi,Peng Wang,Jundong Li,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeqMem-Eval，一个受持续学习启发的诊断评估框架，用于细粒度评估大语言模型在序列推理中的外部记忆质量，揭示传统聚合指标（如最终准确率或累积性能）无法捕捉的遗忘和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆评估主要依赖最终保留准确率或累积在线性能等聚合指标，这些指标会掩盖遗忘和负迁移等关键失效模式，无法全面反映记忆质量。

Method: 提出SeqMem-Eval诊断评估框架，针对测试时外部记忆（提示介导、不修改模型参数）的序列演化场景，测量在线效用、保留泛化能力、反向迁移和遗忘程度四个维度。

Result: 实验表明，更高的最终或累积准确率并不等同于更好的记忆质量；许多方法在性能提升的同时存在严重的遗忘或负迁移；不同记忆设计在适应性与稳定性之间存在传统指标无法揭示的权衡。

Conclusion: SeqMem-Eval提供了比传统聚合指标更细粒度的记忆质量视图，揭示了LLM外部记忆方法在序列推理中的真实行为与权衡。

Abstract: Memory plays a central role in enabling large language models (LLMs) to operate over sequential tasks by accumulating and reusing experience over time. However, existing evaluations of LLM memory mostly rely on aggregate metrics such as final hold-out accuracy or cumulative online performance, which can obscure critical failure modes such as forgetting and negative transfer. In this paper, we introduce SeqMem-Eval, a diagnostic evaluation framework for sequentially evolving LLM memory. Drawing inspiration from continual learning, it targets a test-time setting in which memory is external, prompt-mediated, and updated without modifying model parameters. Rather than focusing only on final performance, SeqMem-Eval evaluates how memory states evolve, generalize, consolidate experience, and retain useful information during sequential inference. Specifically, it measures online utility, hold-out generalization, backward transfer, and forgetting, providing a finer-grained view of memory quality. Through extensive experiments across diverse tasks and memory methods, we show that higher final or cumulative accuracy does not necessarily imply better memory quality: many methods exhibit strong performance gains while suffering from substantial forgetting or negative transfer. Moreover, different memory designs exhibit distinct trade-offs between adaptability and stability that remain invisible under standard evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [161] [Unified High-Probability Analysis of Stochastic Variance-Reduced Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15388)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,M. Berk Sahin,Sang Bin Moon,Anuran Makur,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的随机方差缩减估计框架，基于记忆保持、重置概率和迭代移动修正三部分递归，导出了统一的概率高界，并应用到约束优化中取得了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$的oracle复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机方差缩减估计器（如动量、SPIDER、STORM、PAGE）的分析局限于特定估计器且基于期望，缺乏能揭示其结构权衡的统一理论。

Method: 基于记忆保持、重置概率和迭代移动修正的三分量递归框架，结合新的无维向量值Freedman不等式，在光滑赋范空间中推导统一高概率界。

Result: 1) 统一的概率高界，适用于欧氏和非欧氏设定；2) 镜面下降法的高概率oracle复杂度，具有对置信水平的对数依赖；3) 期望约束随机优化的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$oracle复杂度，优于已有的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4})$。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅能恢复经典估计器、衍生新的二阶变体，还能在约束优化中首次利用方差缩减估计显著改进复杂度界。

Abstract: Stochastic estimators are fundamental to large-scale optimization, where population quantities must be inferred from noisy oracle observations. Although influential methods such as momentum, SPIDER, STORM, and PAGE have been highly successful, their analyses are largely estimator-specific and expectation-based, obscuring the structural tradeoffs that determine reliability. In this paper, we develop a unified framework for stochastic variance-reduced estimation based on a recursion with three components: memory retention, reset probability, and a correction term for iterate movement. This framework recovers several classical estimators, motivates new second-order variants, and yields a bias-variance decomposition of estimation error. Our main result is a unified high-probability bound proved using a new dimension-free vector-valued Freedman inequality, valid for smooth normed spaces involving random sums of vector martingales. The result applies in both Euclidean and non-Euclidean settings, including the analysis of mirror-descent-based methods in Banach spaces. As applications, we obtain high-probability oracle complexities for unconstrained optimization with mirror descent, establishing the logarithmic dependence on the confidence level. We also derive the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ oracle-complexity bounds for stochastic optimization with expectation constraints, improving upon the existing $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4})$ complexity by leveraging variance-reduced estimation for the first time in this setting.

</details>


### [162] [LPDS: Evaluating LLM Robustness Through Logic-Preserving Difficulty Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15393)
*Philipp Mondorf,Samuel J. Bell,Jesse Dodge,Dieuwke Hupkes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑保持难度缩放（LPDS）框架，系统量化问题变体的难度并搜索最易导致模型失败的变体。实验表明，LPDS能高效发现高难度变体，导致性能下降幅度是随机采样的5倍，且在困难变体上微调比在简单变体上训练能带来更一致的鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在逻辑不变但实体（如名称、数字等）变化时容易失败，现有评估缺乏系统方法识别哪些逻辑保持变体最容易引发失败，常依赖随机采样从而高估模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出LPDS框架，包含两个步骤：(i) 量化问题变体的难度；(ii) 系统搜索允许变体空间以找到最大化难度并暴露失败的变体。

Result: 难度增加导致性能下降和推理链错误更明显；LPDS高效发现高难度变体，性能下降幅度是随机采样的5倍；在困难变体上微调比在简单变体上微调带来更一致的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: LPDS框架能有效识别LLM在逻辑保持变体下的失败模式，为评估和提升模型鲁棒性提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to perform tasks with minimal human oversight, it is crucial that these models operate robustly. In particular, a model that can solve a given problem should not fail simply because certain entities$\unicode{x2013}$such as names, numbers, or other contextual details$\unicode{x2013}$have changed while the underlying problem logic remains the same. Prior work suggests that current LLMs still struggle with this form of robustness: they often succeed on some variations of a problem but fail on others. However, existing evaluations often lack a systematic way to identify which logic-preserving variations are most likely to induce failure. Instead, they typically test a random subset of allowable variations, which can overstate robustness. To address this gap, we introduce logic-preserving difficulty scaling (LPDS), a framework that (i) quantifies the difficulty of a problem variation and (ii) systematically searches the space of allowable variations to find those that maximize difficulty and expose failures. We show that as difficulty increases, performance declines and errors in the models' reasoning chains become more pronounced. We further demonstrate that LPDS efficiently finds difficult problem variations for a model, resulting in performance drops up to 5 times larger compared to random sampling. Finally, we show that fine-tuning on more difficult variations leads to more consistent robustness gains than training on easier ones.

</details>


### [163] [SAFE Quantum Machine Learning with Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16067)
*Ying Chen,Paolo Giudici,Vasily Kolesnikov,Paolo Recchia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过振幅编码和可学习经典预编码层增强的变分量子分类器，结合SAFE-AI评估框架，在安全关键场景下展现出更平衡的可靠性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索变分量子电路在高维深度表示下的分类能力，并解决量子模型在安全性关键应用中的可靠性评估问题。

Method: 采用振幅编码将数据嵌入高维量子态，结合归一化振幅嵌入与有界量子可观测量，构建结构化的平滑假设空间；引入可学习的经典预编码层进行稳定化处理；使用基于Cramer von Mises散度的SAFE-AI指标从准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性三个维度评估模型。

Result: 所提出的量子模型在预测性能上与强大的经典基线相当，但在SAFE可靠性方面表现更均衡，对噪声具有更好的鲁棒性，且在结构化特征移除下保持稳定性。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路为安全关键场景下的稳定导向SAFE学习提供了有原理依据的机制。

Abstract: We propose a variational quantum classifier operating on high dimensional deep representations via amplitude encoding, stabilized by a learnable classical pre encoding layer.By combining normalized amplitude embeddings with bounded quantum observables, the resulting model induces a structured and smooth hypothesis class with controlled sensitivity to input variations. Model reliability is assessed using SAFE-AI metrics derived from the Cramer von Mises divergence, enabling consistent evaluation across accuracy, robustness, and explainability dimensions. Empirical results show that the proposed quantum model provides competitive predictive performance compared with strong classical baselines while exhibiting a more balanced SAFE reliability profile, with improved robustness to noise and stability under structured feature removal. These findings suggest that variational quantum circuits offer a principled mechanism for stability oriented SAFE learning in safety critical settings.

</details>


### [164] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 差分隐私改变了CVaR学习的有效样本量，隐私相关的样本量不是n而是nτ，有效私有尾样本量为εnτ。私有CVaR超额风险分解为普通尾风险统计误差和隐私代价。标量估计和有限类分别达到Θ(B min{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}})和Θ(B min{1,√(log(2M)/(nτ))+log(2M)/(εnτ)}}的完整速率。凸Lipschitz学习中，CVaR特定隐私项必须以1/(εnτ)尺度缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究差分隐私约束下条件风险价值（CVaR）学习问题的有效样本量变化，揭示隐私保护对尾部风险学习的影响机制。

Method: 通过理论分析将私有CVaR超额风险分解为普通尾风险统计误差和隐私代价两部分；对三种设定（标量估计、有限类、凸Lipschitz学习）分别推导上下界，建立完整的最优速率。

Result: 标量估计和有限类在纯DP下获得完整的最优速率；凸Lipschitz学习中隐私项必然以1/(εnτ)尺度缩放，维度依赖继承自私有随机凸优化。

Conclusion: 私有CVaR学习的核心困难子问题等价于在Θ(nτ)个信息性尾部记录上进行普通私有学习。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [165] [Breakeven complexity: A new perspective on neural partial differential equation solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15399)
*Yijing Zhang,Nicholas Roberts,Tanya Marwah,Mikhail Khodak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以"盈亏平衡复杂度"为核心指标的学习型PDE求解器评估框架，通过考虑数据生成、训练调优等前期成本，对比神经求解器与传统求解器的端到端效率，发现在高难度问题场景下神经求解器更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于精度的评估忽略了神经求解器高昂的前期成本（数据生成、训练、调优），也未考虑传统求解器在低成本下也能提供低精度解，无法真实反映学习型求解器的实际效用。

Method: 提出"盈亏平衡复杂度"（breakeven complexity）指标，统计使学习型求解器成本效益超过误差等效的传统求解器所需的正向求解次数；结合标度律（scaling laws）分配数据生成预算，并实现不同场景下的平滑误差匹配。

Result: 在APEBench的2D周期域三个PDE以及基于PyFR的流经多障碍物基准上评估多个神经PDE求解器，发现神经求解器在问题难度（成本、维度、 rollout长度、物理状态如高雷诺数）增加时变得更有效。

Conclusion: 学习型PDE求解器的实用性应基于端到端成本效益评估，而非单纯精度；其优势在复杂高难度问题中更为显著。

Abstract: Neural surrogate solvers of partial differential equations (PDEs) promise dramatic speedups over numerical methods, especially in scenarios requiring many solves. However, current accuracy-based evaluations do not fully consider two central issues: (1) neural solvers incur substantial up-front costs for data generation, training, and tuning; and (2) classical solvers can also generate low-fidelity solutions at a sufficiently low simulation cost. To explicitly account for these realities and fully incorporate end-to-end costs, we propose an evaluation framework centered on breakeven complexity, a metric that counts the forward solves before a learned solver is cost-effective relative to an error-equivalent traditional solver. To evaluate this measure, we apply scaling laws to determine how much training budget to allocate to data generation and discuss how to achieve smooth error-matching in diverse settings. We evaluate the breakeven complexity of multiple neural PDE solvers on three PDEs on 2D periodic domains from APEBench and a novel benchmark of flows past multiple obstacles generated by the GPU-native PyFR code. Among other findings, our results suggest that neural PDE solvers become more effective as problems get harder in terms of cost, dimension, rollout, physics regime (e.g. higher Reynolds number), etc.

</details>


### [166] [Transformer Scalability Crisis: The First Comprehensive Empirical Analysis of Performance Walls in Modern Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15413)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对118个transformer模型在7种架构类别上进行大规模系统评估，揭示了严重的可扩展性危机：序列长度从512 tokens到2048 tokens时，模型成功率从88.1%骤降至0%。压缩模型在参数效率上（649.2 tokens/sec/M）远优于大型生成模型（12.5 tokens/sec/M），首次定量证明了理论上的O(n²)注意力复杂度在实际中表现为可测量的性能墙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: transformer架构的扩展性限制缺乏系统性实证分析，现有研究多关注性能提升而忽略了其扩展瓶颈的定量评估。

Method: 对118个transformer模型（涵盖7种架构类别）进行大规模基准测试，在序列长度128到2048 tokens范围内系统评估加载时间、内存消耗和计算效率，并分析参数效率指标。

Result: 88.1%模型在512 tokens时成功，44.9%在1024 tokens，0%在2048 tokens；压缩模型参数效率（649.2 tokens/sec/M）远超大型生成模型（12.5 tokens/sec/M）。

Conclusion: transformer存在由O(n²)注意力复杂度导致的硬性性能墙，挑战了现有的扩展假设，为生产环境的部署决策提供了关键定量依据，并建立了新的基准评估方法论。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of transformer architectures in natural language processing, their scalability limitations remain poorly understood through systematic empirical analysis. This paper presents the first comprehensive large-scale evaluation of 118 transformer models across seven distinct architectural categories, revealing fundamental performance walls that manifest as hard deployment constraints. Our systematic benchmarking methodology uncovers a critical scalability crisis: while 88.1% of models successfully process sequences up to 512 tokens, this drops dramatically to 44.9% at 1024 tokens, with complete failure (0%) at 2048 tokens. Through rigorous analysis of loading times, memory consumption, and computational efficiency across sequence lengths from 128 to 2048 tokens, we demonstrate that compressed models achieve superior parameter efficiency (649.2 tokens/sec/M parameters) compared to large generative models (12.5 tokens/sec/M). Our findings challenge prevailing scaling assumptions and provide the first quantitative evidence that the theoretical O(n2) attention complexity translates into measurable performance walls. This work establishes new benchmarking methodologies for transformer evaluation and provides critical insights for practical deployment decisions in production environments.

</details>


### [167] [Margin-Adaptive Confidence Ranking for Reliable LLM Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15416)
*Gaojie Jin,Yong Tao,Lijia Yu,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM与人类判断的一致性保证问题，提出通过学习专用置信度估计器替代启发式置信度信号，利用模拟标注者多样性和基于边界的排序公式显式建模模型区分人类一致与不一致案例的能力，并推导出该估计器的泛化保证，揭示了边界相关的权衡关系，从而在固定序列测试中提高排序准确率和一致性成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM与人类判断一致性保证框架依赖于模型估计的置信度与人类不一致风险单调相关的假设，但该假设在实践中可能被违反，且置信度估计器的泛化行为未被明确分析。

Method: 1) 学习专用置信度估计器替代启发式置信度信号；2) 利用模拟标注者多样性和基于边界的排序公式显式建模LLM区分人类一致与不一致案例的能力；3) 推导该估计器的泛化保证，揭示边界相关的权衡关系；4) 设计自适应估计器训练过程，集成到固定序列测试中。

Result: 学习到的置信度估计器在固定序列测试中提高了排序准确率，增强了置信度与不一致风险之间的单调关系，在多个数据集和评判模型上实现了更高的目标一致性水平满足成功率。

Conclusion: 通过学习专用置信度估计器并引入泛化保证，有效缓解了原有框架中单调性假设被违反的问题，提升了LLM与人类判断一致性保证的可靠性和成功率。

Abstract: Jung et al. (2025) introduce a hypothesis testing framework for guaranteeing agreement between large language models (LLMs) and human judgments, relying on the assumption that the model's estimated confidence is monotonic with respect to human-disagreement risk. In practice, however, this assumption may be violated, and the generalization behavior of the confidence estimator is not explicitly analyzed. We mitigate these issues by learning a dedicated confidence estimator instead of relying on heuristic confidence signals. Our approach leverages simulated annotator diversity and a margin-based ranking formulation to explicitly model how confidently an LLM distinguishes between human-agreement and human-disagreement cases. We further derive generalization guarantees for this estimator, revealing a margin-dependent trade-off that informs the design of an adaptive estimator training procedure. When integrated into fixed-sequence testing, the learned confidence estimator yields improved ranking accuracy and empirically strengthens the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk, leading to higher success rates in satisfying target agreement levels across multiple datasets and judge models.

</details>


### [168] [Lagrangian Flow Matching: A Least-Action Framework for Principled Path Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15419)
*Shukai Du,Junzhe Zhang,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日流匹配（Lagrangian Flow Matching），将概率路径和速度场的设计推广到一般拉格朗日作用量最小化框架，涵盖现有直线路径和扩散路径作为特例，并导出仿真-free的训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法（如Rectified Flow、OT-based Flow Matching）仅使用直线路径连接耦合端点，对应经典力学中最简单的自由粒子情况，动力学类型受限。作者希望将概率路径设计推广到更一般的物理动力学框架。

Method: 提出Lagrangian Flow Matching框架：在连续性方程和端点约束下，通过最小化一般拉格朗日量的作用量来确定概率路径和速度场。证明该动态问题等价于静态最优传输问题，从而得到仿真-free的训练目标。动能拉格朗日恢复OT-based流匹配，谐振子拉格朗日恢复trigonometric VPsde扩散路径。

Result: 数值实验表明，更一般的拉格朗日量能产生有意义的动力学变化，同时与现有条件流匹配模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日流匹配为概率路径设计提供了统一的物理视角，将流匹配与最优传输、经典力学联系起来，开创了一类新的仿真-free生成模型。

Abstract: Flow matching trains a neural velocity field by regression against a target velocity associated with a prescribed probability path connecting a simple initial distribution to the data distribution. A central design choice is the path itself. Existing constructions, including rectified and optimal-transport-based paths, transport samples along straight lines between coupled endpoints and thus cover only a narrow class of dynamics. We observe that this corresponds to the simplest case of the least-action principle in classical mechanics, in which the kinetic Lagrangian yields free-particle straight-line trajectories. Building on this observation, we propose Lagrangian flow matching, a physics-based framework in which the probability path and velocity field are determined by minimizing the action of a general Lagrangian subject to the continuity equation and the prescribed endpoints. We show that this dynamic problem admits an equivalent static optimal transport (OT) formulation, yielding a family of simulation-free training objectives that recover OT-based flow matching as the kinetic special case and the trigonometric variance-preserving diffusion path as the harmonic-oscillator case. More general Lagrangians give rise to new probability paths and velocity fields, and numerical experiments show that they induce meaningful changes in the learned dynamics while remaining competitive with existing conditional flow matching models.

</details>


### [169] [Spectral Priors vs. Attention: Investigating the Utility of Attention Mechanisms in EEG-Based Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15433)
*Tawsik Jawad,Gowtham Atluri,Vikram Ravindra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频谱选择性特征构建的方法，通过提取脑电信号中主要脑波频带的信号强度，将高维原始数据转化为高价值频谱特征。实验表明，基于频域和时频域特征的传统机器学习模型能够达到或超越SOTA深度学习模型性能，而注意力机制无法提取稳定特征签名，且其局限性本质上是根本性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG时间序列信号存在显著噪声和粗空间分辨率，导致神经退行性疾病分类困难。即使是SOTA深度学习架构也难以区分健康对照与患病受试者或不同疾病类型，因为组间相似性高。

Method: 采用频谱选择性特征构建方法，隔离主要脑波频带内的信号强度，将高维原始数据转换为高价值频谱特征。使用频域和时频域特征，结合传统机器学习模型。

Result: 1) 频域和时频域特征使传统机器学习模型匹配或超越SOTA深度学习模型；2) 注意力机制无法提取表征健康神经活动的稳定特征签名；3) 即使提供频率选择性的时域输入，注意力模型的性能也无明显提升，表明其局限性是根本性的。

Conclusion: 频谱选择性特征构建方法能有效增强EEG分类中的类别可分性，注意力机制在处理频谱特征方面存在根本性局限，传统机器学习模型在适当特征工程下可达到更优性能。

Abstract: Electroencephalograph (EEG) timeseries signals are characterized by significant noise and coarse spatial resolution, which complicates the classification of neurodegenerative diseases. Even SOTA deep learning architectures struggle to distinguish between healthy controls and diseased subjects, or between different disease types, due to high intergroup similarity. In this paper, we show that a spectrally selective approach to feature construction enhances class separability. By isolating signal strengths within the primary brainwave bands, we transform high dimensional raw data into high value spectral features. Our results demonstrate that a) features derived from frequency and time frequency domain allow traditional machine learning models to match or exceed the performance of SOTA deep learning models, b) Attention mechanism is unable to distill the stable feature signatures that characterize healthy neural activity in both resting and task EEGs, and c) the limitations of attention based models in finding relevant spectral features appear to be fundamental in that providing frequency selective time domain input do not appreciably improve their performance. We validate our methodology across three open source resting EEG datasets and one task EEG dataset, providing robust empirical evidence for our claims.

</details>


### [170] [DrugSAGE:Self-evolving Agent Experience for Efficient State-of-the-Art Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15461)
*Yikun Zhang,Xiwei Cheng,Tianyu Liu,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEAx框架，通过跨任务经验记忆累积与复用来高效构建药物发现SOTA预测模型，在33个分子性质预测任务中排名第一，并在零测试时搜索场景下超越基线10-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体通过大量试错寻找SOTA方案，但每次新任务都需要从头开始搜索，成本高昂且无法保留先前积累的经验。

Method: SEAx（Self-evolving Agent Experience）框架，维护跨任务记忆，包含已验证的技能、有效策略的统计证据、常见错误及其修复记录，支持直接迁移解决方案而无需测试时搜索。

Result: 在33个分子性质预测任务中单任务设置排名第一；从16个较小任务积累记忆后，在17个保留任务上平均归一化得分0.935，零测试时搜索场景下超越所有基线10-30%。

Conclusion: 跨任务记忆能显著提升药物发现中SOTA模型开发的效率，展示了经验复用的优势。

Abstract: Building state-of-the-art (SOTA) predictive models for drug discovery requires expensive search over tools, architectures, and training strategies. Current LLM-based agents can find SOTA solutions through extensive trial and error, but they do not retain the experience accumulated along the way and therefore pay the full search cost on every new task. We propose \method (Self-evolving Agent Experience), a framework that accumulates and reuses experience across tasks to build SOTA drug discovery models efficiently. \method maintains a cross-task memory of verified skills, statistical evidence about effective strategies, and a record of recurring errors and their fixes. In some cases, \method transfers a working solution directly without test-time search. In 33 molecular property prediction tasks, \method ranks first among nine SOTA agents in a single-task setting. With memory accumulated from 16 smaller tasks, \method achieves an averaged normalized score of 0.935 on 17 held-out tasks in a cross-task evaluation setting and outperforms all baseline agents by 10-30\% in a zero-test-time search regime. In summary, our work shows the advantage of cross-task memory for efficient SOTA model development in drug discovery.

</details>


### [171] [Toward World Modeling of Physiological Signals with Chaos-Theoretic Balancing and Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15465)
*Yunfei Luo,Xi Chen,Yuliang Chen,Lanshuang Zhang,Md Mofijul Islam,Siwei Zhao,Peter Kotanko,Subhasis Dasgupta,Andrew Campbell,Rakesh Malhotra,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NormWear-2 是一个生理时间序列世界模型，通过将多变量生理信号与临床干预变量编码到共享潜空间并建模其联合时间演化，实现了跨多时间尺度的长期预测。该方法融合预训练直觉与非参数潜状态自适应，并发现混沌理论驱动的动态平衡可提升表征鲁棒性。在涵盖8026名受试者的多场景数据集上，该模型在时域、频域和潜表示域均取得最佳预测性能，优于现有时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理时间序列研究集中于静态分类、事件预测或短时下一步预测，长期信号级预测及生理信号的预测性本质仍未被充分探索。

Method: NormWear-2 将多变量生理信号与临床干预变量编码到共享潜空间，建模其联合时间演化动力学；结合预训练先验知识（直觉推理）与即时非参数潜状态转移适应（洞察力）；预训练阶段采用混沌理论平衡动力学模式多样性以增强表征鲁棒性。

Result: 在涵盖8026名受试者、多种时间分辨率和干预场景（日常生活、即时护理、临床环境）的数据集上，NormWear-2 在时域、频域和潜表示域均取得最优整体预测性能，显著优于最先进的时间序列基础模型，同时保持有竞争力的下游表征质量。

Conclusion: NormWear-2 是迈向通用生理信号世界模型的一步，展示了长期预测能力与表征质量的平衡，且混沌平衡的较小语料库可优于两倍大小的非平衡语料库并捕获分岔动力学。

Abstract: Physiological time series signals reflect complex, multi-scale dynamical processes of the human body. Existing modeling studies focus on static tasks such as classification, event forecasting, or short-horizon next step prediction, while long-horizon signal-level forecasting and predictive nature of physiological signals remain underexplored. We introduce NormWear-2, a world model that encodes both multivariate physiological signals and clinical intervention variables into a shared latent space and models their joint temporal evolution as a dynamical system. Our approach combines inference from prior pre-trained knowledge (intuition) with instant non-parametric latent state transition adaptation (insight), enabling coherent forecasting across multiple temporal scales, conditioned on heterogeneous clinical interventions. During the pretraining phase, we find that chaos-theoretic balancing of dynamical regime diversity yields more robust representations, with a smaller balanced corpus outperforming one twice its size and capturing bifurcation regimes. We evaluate the world model performance across diverse real-world physiological datasets spanning heterogeneous temporal resolutions and intervention regimes, covering daily life, point-of-care, and clinical settings, including fitness planning, hemodialysis, diabetes management, and surgical monitoring. These evaluation datasets comprise records from 8,026 subjects, spanning study durations from 3.2 hours for high-resolution signal data to 2.3 years for longitudinal clinical biomarker tracking. NormWear-2 achieves the best overall forecasting performance across time, frequency, and latent representation domains, with significant improvements over state-of-the-art time series foundation models, while maintaining competitive downstream representation quality, providing a step toward general-purpose world models for physiological signals.

</details>


### [172] [parallelcbf: A composable safety-filter and auditability framework for tensor-parallel reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15509)
*Yijun Lu,Zilei Yang,Yuyin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParallelCBF 是首个统一张量并行无人机环境、硬门控CBF安全滤波器、分片BC-to-RL流水线和可组合审计API的框架，支持端到端安全约束训练。框架通过了39项属性测试（1.67秒），并通过审计层阻止了不符合预注册收敛标准的退化检查点传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（Isaac Lab、OmniSafe、safe-control-gym、CBFKit）各自提供无人机仿真、约束强化学习基准或控制屏障函数工具，但缺乏一个统一的端到端安全约束训练框架。

Method: 四层可组合API设计：(i) 张量并行无人机环境；(ii) 硬门控CBF安全滤波器，包含双屏障（平方/线性预测）CBF的CPU PyTorch参考实现；(iii) 分片BC-to-RL流水线；(iv) 一等操作性审计能力（预注册、看门狗注册表、故障取证、数据集审计）。

Result: 完整39项属性测试套件在向量化批大小上1.67秒完成；31,415幕行为克隆采集活动，其课程混合、逐桶产量和数据集SHA-256可通过框架ops原语审计；审计层成功阻止未达预注册收敛标准的退化检查点传播。

Conclusion: ParallelCBF 证明了审计能力作为可组合API而非用户脚本的内建架构特性，对于可复现的实证机器人研究是必要的。

Abstract: While Isaac Lab provides massive parallel UAV simulation, OmniSafe and safe-control-gym provide constrained-RL benchmarks, and CBFKit provides control-barrier-function synthesis tooling, no existing framework unifies these capabilities for end-to-end safety-constrained training. ParallelCBF is the first framework to unify (i)~tensor-parallel UAV environments, (ii)~hard-gate CBF safety filters, (iii)~sharded BC-to-RL pipelines, and (iv)~first-class operational auditability -- pre-registration, watchdog registries, failure forensics, and dataset audits as composable APIs rather than user-implemented scripts. We release ParallelCBF v0.1.0 under Apache~2.0 with a four-layer composable API, a CPU PyTorch reference implementation of a dual-barrier (squared / linear-predictive) CBF, property-based safety invariance tests across vectorized batch sizes that complete in 1.67~s for the full 39-test suite, and a 31{,}415-episode behavior-cloning collection campaign whose curriculum mix, per-bucket yields, and dataset SHA-256 are auditable through the framework's own \texttt{ops} primitives. We report a representative end-to-end pipeline execution in which the framework's auditability layer halted a downstream training stage that did not meet pre-registered convergence criteria, preventing silent propagation of a degraded checkpoint -- an architectural property we argue is necessary, not merely useful, for reproducible empirical robotics research. The framework is installable via \texttt{pip install parallelcbf}; source and release artifacts are available at https://github.com/xiaoyang-123-cell/ParallelCBF.

</details>


### [173] [OgBench: A Framework for Evaluating Graph Neural Networks on Omics Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15511)
*Louisa Cornelis,Johan Mathe,Louis Van Langendonck,Guillermo Bernárdez,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OgBench——首个针对组学数据中n≪p（样本少、节点多）场景的图分类基准平台。通过标准化流程将原始组学数据转化为带特征图，实验发现常用GNN在此场景下往往不如简单MLP和经典基线方法，挑战了图结构在此领域必然有价值的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图分类基准大多集中在n ≫ p（图数量远多于每图节点数）的设定，忽视了生物组学领域中n ≪ p（样本少、每样本节点多）的实际情况，因此需要专门评估GNN在此场景下的表现。

Method: 构建OgBench平台：提供从原始组学数据到带特征图的端到端标准化流程，生成不同结构属性的图族；在n≪p设定下系统评测经典GNN、面向大图的GNN、面向组学的GNN，并与MLP及经典机器学习基线进行对比。

Result: 常用GNN在组学n≪p场景下往往无法超越简单的MLP和经典基线方法，表明图结构在此领域并不天然带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 该发现挑战了图结构在组学领域必然有价值的假设，促进对当前学习范式的批判性反思；OgBench为社区提供了开源生态，以开发和验证专门面向生物图的新型架构。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become the dominant framework for inductive graph-level learning. Yet most benchmarks focus on the regime $n \gg p$, where the number of graphs $n$ greatly exceeds the number of nodes per graph $p$. This overlooks biological domains such as omics, which operate in the opposite $n \ll p$ regime, characterized by large graphs of genes, transcripts, or proteins across few patient samples. This raises the question: \textit{how do GNNs perform in this low-sample, high-node omics setting?} We introduce \texttt{OgBench} (Omics-Graph Bench), the first benchmarking platform for graph-level prediction in the $n \ll p$ regime characteristic of omics data. We provide a standardized, end-to-end modular infrastructure from raw omics data to families of featured graphs with varied structural properties. We benchmark classical GNNs, as well as GNNs designed for large graphs and omics applications, alongside MLPs and machine learning baselines to establish reference performances. Our results show that widely used GNNs often do not outperform simple MLPs and classical baselines. These findings challenge the prevailing assumption that graph structure inherently adds value in this domain, fostering a critical reassessment of current learning paradigms. Ultimately, by exposing these limitations, OgBench provides the open-source ecosystem necessary for the community to develop and validate novel architectures explicitly tailored for biological graphs. The code is available at https://github.com/geometric-intelligence/ogbench.

</details>


### [174] [On the Fragility of Data Attribution When Learning Is Distributed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15520)
*Xian Gao,Bo Hui,Min-Te Sun,Wei-Shinn Ku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现数据归因方法在分布式训练中易受攻击：单个参与者可通过注入少量合成数据批次，在保持全局效用的同时大幅提升自身归因值，并扭曲其他良性客户的归因结构。这表明归因本身构成了新的攻击面，亟需开发鲁棒的评分机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据归因方法隐式假设归因值能忠实反映参与者的贡献，但这一假设是否成立尚未被充分验证。作者希望揭示归因机制本身存在的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出"归因优先攻击"（attribution-first attack），利用潜在优化（latent optimization）注入微小的合成数据批次，利用非独立同分布（non-IID）的标签覆盖和评估器敏感性来提升攻击者的归因值，同时保持全局效用不变。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和边际效用评估器下，该攻击持续提升攻击者的归因值，重塑良性客户间的相对归因结构，且不降低模型准确率，也不触发基于几何结构的防御机制。

Conclusion: 归因本身构成了新的攻击面，现有归因方法缺乏鲁棒性，需要开发具有归因鲁棒性和激励兼容性的评分机制。

Abstract: Data attribution has become an important component of pricing, auditing, and governance in machine learning pipelines, yet most attribution methods implicitly assume that attribution values faithfully reflect participants' contributions. We show that this assumption can fail: a single participant in a standard distributed training workflow can substantially inflate its measured attribution value while preserving global utility. Our attribution-first attack uses latent optimization to inject small synthetic batches that preserve utility while exploiting non-IID label coverage and evaluator sensitivities. Across datasets, models, and multiple marginal-utility evaluators, the attack consistently increases the adversary's attribution value and reshapes the relative attribution structure among benign clients without degrading accuracy or triggering geometry-based defenses. These results show that attribution itself forms a new attack surface and motivate the development of attribution-robust and incentive-compatible scoring mechanisms.

</details>


### [175] [Neural Point-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15524)
*Bruno Trentini,Jacob Hume,Vincenzo Antonio Isoldi,Philipp Misof,Ekaterina S. Ivshina,Kelly Maggs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经点形式（NPFs），一种利用扩散几何中的拉普拉斯技术来构建点云上微分形式比较的新颖可学习几何特征。通过将子流形表示为比较矩阵并证明其在标准假设下的一致性，NPFs提供了紧凑、高效且排列不变的神经层，在合成与生物实验中展现出竞争力和可解释性，尤其在标签依赖于采样密度、流形结构或群体几何时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云学习常假设观测样本是底层几何对象（如高维特征空间中的流形）的带噪迹线，但现有方法（坐标、成对距离、学习图邻域）无法直接捕获高阶切向信息（即微分形式所编码的几何信息）。因此需要一种能表示点云上高阶切向几何特征的可学习方法。

Method: 利用扩散几何中的拉普拉斯技术，在没有自然切触结构的情况下，通过内积构建点云上微分形式比较的离散模型。将共享环境空间中的子流形表示为比较矩阵，矩阵元素描述特征形式对与外在切向信息的交互方式。证明了在标准采样、带宽、密度和流形假设下比较矩阵的长期一致性，并由此构建紧凑、高效、排列不变的神经层（NPFs），输出学习到的形式比较矩阵。

Result: 在合成和生物相关实验中，NPFs提供了具有竞争力和可解释性的表示，其最强优势体现在标签依赖于采样密度、流形结构或响应相关群体几何的场景中。

Conclusion: NPFs作为一种新颖的、基于原则的点云几何特征学习方法，有效编码了微分形式所代表的高阶切向信息，并在多种任务中展现优越性能，特别适合处理具有底层流形结构的数据。

Abstract: Point cloud learning often rests on the premise that observed samples are noisy traces of an underlying geometric object, such as a manifold embedded in a high-dimensional feature space. Yet much of this geometry is not captured directly by coordinates, pairwise distances, or learned graph neighborhoods alone. In the smooth setting, differential forms are devices to encode higher order tangency information. In this work, we introduce a new family of principled learnable geometric features for point clouds called neural point-forms (NPFs). In the absence of a natural tangency structure, we instead use Laplacian-based techniques from Diffusion Geometry to build a discrete model for comparing differential forms on point clouds via inner products. In the continuum, submanifolds of a shared ambient feature space are represented as comparison matrices, whose entries describe how pairs of feature forms interact with extrinsic tangency information. We make this intuition precise by proving the long-run consistency of comparison matrices under standard sampling, bandwidth, density, and manifold-hypothesis assumptions. This yields a compact, efficient and permutation-invariant neural layer whose output is a learned form-comparison matrix. Across synthetic and biologically relevant experiments, we show that NPFs provide a competitive, and interpretable representation, with the strongest benefits appearing when labels depend on sampling density, manifold-like structure, or response-relevant population geometry.

</details>


### [176] [DeltaPrompts: Escaping the Zero-Delta Trap in Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15532)
*Jaehun Jung,Hyunwoo Kim,Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有VLM蒸馏策略中高达69%的提示是零差（zero-delta），即教师与学生输出分布一致，无法提供有效学习信号。本文提出DeltaPrompts，通过量化师生间的答案差异（Δ），主动生成高分歧的合成推理问题数据集（200k），在多种设定下显著提升模型性能（最高15%相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示标准蒸馏策略中存在的"零差陷阱"（zero-delta trap）：大量训练提示下师生输出无差异，导致蒸馏收益迅速饱和。

Method: 1) 量化师生间的答案差异（Δ），证明非零分歧对有效缩放至关重要；2) 提出分阶段合成流水线，以现有数据集为种子，主动针对学生失败模式生成高分歧推理问题；3) 构建包含20万样本的DeltaPrompts数据集。

Result: 在on-policy蒸馏、跨模型族迁移、off-policy微调三种设定下，DeltaPrompts均带来显著提升，即使在高度优化的推理模型（Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking）上仍取得最高15%相对提升，覆盖10个图表、文档及感知推理基准。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦师生功能差距而非简单数据规模扩展，可以更有效地驱动蒸馏过程，为VLM蒸馏的数据筛选与生成提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Distillation enables compact Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to obtain strong reasoning capabilities, yet the prompts driving this process are typically chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets. We reveal a critical inefficiency in this approach: up to 69% of the prompts in standard chart / document reasoning datasets are effectively zero-delta, meaning the teacher and student already induce the exact same answer distribution. Training on these prompts provides minimal learning signal, causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale. To escape the zero-delta trap, we return to first principles: distillation fundamentally minimizes distributional divergence, and thus a prompt is valuable only if it exposes a functional capability gap between the teacher and student. We quantify this gap through answer divergence ($Δ$), demonstrating that non-zero divergence is critical for effective scaling. Building on this insight, we propose a staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds, actively targeting student failure modes to produce better prompts. The result is DeltaPrompts, a diverse dataset of 200k synthetic, high-divergence reasoning problems. We evaluate DeltaPrompts across three distinct settings: on-policy distillation with the target teacher-student pair, transfer to a novel model family without regenerating the data, and off-policy fine-tuning of a non-reasoning model. Across all scenarios, DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains, yielding up to 15% relative improvement even on top of a highly-optimized reasoning model (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) -- averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document and perception-centric reasoning.

</details>


### [177] [CTF4Nuclear: Common Task Framework for Nuclear Fission and Fusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15549)
*Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,Antonio Cammi,Dean Price,Alexey Yermakov,Yue Zhao,Philippe M. Wyder,Judah Goldfeder,Jan Williams,Amy Sara Rude,Matteo Tomasetto,Joe Germany,Joseph Bakarji,Georg Maierhofer,Miles Cranmer,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于核工程领域机器学习方法的通用任务框架（CTF），该框架基于精选的多源数据集、12项标准指标以及稀疏测量下的系统监测新范式，对多种ML基线方法进行了基准测试，旨在通过标准化评估提升核科学ML的严谨性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核能系统设计运行复杂，高保真仿真计算昂贵且不适于实时应用，传统模型方法对简化假设敏感，导致与真实测量存在偏差；机器学习虽有望构建快速可靠的替代模型，但可用的数据驱动方法繁多，在核工程这一安全关键领域中，亟需对不同ML方法进行公平比较并清晰理解其优劣。

Method: 借鉴动力学系统和地震学领域已有工作，构建了一个通用任务框架（CTF），包含来自不同核系统及核相关系统的精选数据集；采用12项既定指标以及一个专注于稀疏测量下系统监测的新评估范式；通过在此框架上对标准ML基线方法进行基准测试来展示框架有效性。

Result: 通过CTF对标准ML基线方法在多个核工程数据集上进行了基准测试，揭示了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 旨在以标准化评估取代临时性对比，在隐藏测试集上评估方法性能，从而提升核工业科学ML研究的严谨性和可重复性。

Abstract: The demand for clean energy is ever increasing, with new nuclear technologies presenting a complementary solution to renewable energies. However, designing and operating these systems is exceptionally difficult, given the complexity of the physical phenomena that interact to form the system dynamics. While high-fidelity simulations help to understand the non-linear, multi-physics interactions within a reactor, they are computationally expensive and rarely suitable for real-time applications. Furthermore, model-based approaches are inherently sensitive to simplifying assumptions required to derive their governing equations and parameters, leading to inevitable discrepancies with real-world measurements. In contrast, Machine Learning (ML) methods have the potential to generate reliable surrogate models which may be able to quickly predict the system's behaviour. However, the number of data-driven methods that can potentially be used for this task is large and diverse. In a safety-critical setting such as nuclear engineering, a fair comparison of different ML methods, and a clear understanding of their advantages and limitations, is of paramount importance. To address this, we introduce a Common Task Framework (CTF) for ML in nuclear engineering, building upon previous efforts in dynamical systems and seismology. This CTF considers a curated set of datasets from different nuclear and nuclear-adjacent systems. The CTF evaluates the performance of a method on 12 established metrics, alongside a new paradigm focused on system monitoring from sparse measurements only. We illustrate the framework by benchmarking standard ML baselines against these datasets, revealing current method limitations. Our vision is to replace ad hoc comparisons with standardized evaluations on hidden test sets, raising the bar for rigour and reproducibility in scientific ML for the nuclear industry.

</details>


### [178] [CrystalBoltz: End-to-End Protein Structure Determination via Experiment-Guided Diffusion for X-Ray Crystallography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15564)
*Minseo Kim,Huanghao Mai,Jay Shenoy,Alec Follmer,Gordon Wetzstein,Frederic Poitevin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CrystalBoltz 是一个生成式框架，将晶体学精修转化为原子结构上的贝叶斯推理，直接基于结构因子振幅操作，通过实验引导的后验采样和原子坐标与B因子精修，在多个蛋白质晶体学数据集上实现了比现有方法更低的坐标RMSD和R因子，同时将运行时间缩短了33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型大多基于公开蛋白质结构数据库（主要为X射线晶体学测定）训练，无法有效利用新晶体学实验的测量数据；晶体学中结构因子振幅无法独立确定电子密度图或原子结构，因为相关相位未被观测到且需要推断，导致结构测定仍需大量人工精修。

Method: CrystalBoltz 将晶体学精修形式化为原子结构上的贝叶斯推理，直接对结构因子振幅进行操作。首先使用预训练的蛋白质结构先验进行无引导生成，然后过渡到实验引导的后验采样，最后进行原子坐标和B因子精修。

Result: 在多个蛋白质晶体学数据集上，CrystalBoltz 相比最强基线方法获得了更低的坐标RMSD和更低的R因子，同时将运行时间减少了33倍。

Conclusion: CrystalBoltz 有效解决了生成模型难以直接利用晶体学实验数据进行结构精修的问题，大幅提升了精修效率和精度。

Abstract: Generative models trained on public databases of protein structures, most of which have been determined by X-ray crystallography, now provide powerful priors for structure prediction. However, they are not readily conditioned on the measurements from a new crystallographic experiment, limiting their use for X-ray structure determination. In crystallography, the measured structure-factor amplitudes do not by themselves determine an electron density map or atomic structure because the associated phases are unobserved and must be inferred. Structure determination therefore remains an inverse problem in which candidate models must be both structurally plausible and consistent with measured diffraction data, often requiring substantial manual refinement by human experts. Emerging methods aim to incorporate experimental information more directly into predictive and refinement workflows. We present CrystalBoltz, a generative framework that casts crystallographic refinement as Bayesian inference over atomic structures and operates directly on structure-factor amplitudes. CrystalBoltz moves from unguided generation with a pre-trained prior over protein structures to experiment-guided posterior sampling, followed by atomic coordinate and B-factor refinement. Across multiple protein crystallography datasets, CrystalBoltz attains lower coordinate RMSD and lower R-factors than the strongest baselines considered, while reducing runtime by a factor of 33 relative to existing experimentally guided refinement.

</details>


### [179] [AstraFlow: Dataflow-Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15565)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Jiahui Wang,Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Z. Morley Mao,Ion Stoica,Jiawei Zhao,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AstraFlow是一个数据流导向的强化学习系统，通过解耦组件抽象解决了现有LLM RL系统中训练器中心化架构带来的扩展性瓶颈，原生支持多策略协作训练、弹性异构跨区域执行，在多种任务上实现2.7倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习系统（如用于提升推理、编码和工具使用能力的RL系统）存在两大问题：一是训练器中心化的控制架构导致每个新扩展都需要专门的系统工程；二是难以高效利用弹性、异构、跨区域的计算资源来支持复杂的智能体RL工作负载（如多策略协作训练），导致RL扩展到智能体LLM时成本过高。

Method: AstraFlow采用数据流导向（dataflow-oriented）的系统设计，用原则化的组件抽象替代传统的训练器中心化控制架构。系统将三个核心功能解耦为自治组件：rollout服务（数据采集）、数据流管理（数据处理与分发）和训练（模型更新）。这种解耦使得系统原生支持多策略（multi-policy）智能体RL工作负载，并能高效利用多样化的计算资源。

Result: 在数学、代码、搜索和AgentBench等多个基准任务上，AstraFlow在不修改系统级代码的情况下，支持了多策略训练、弹性扩展、异构跨区域执行和可组合的数据算法。在多策略协作训练中，AstraFlow达到了与现有RL系统相当或更优的准确率，同时训练速度提升了2.7倍。

Conclusion: 数据流导向的组件抽象范式能有效克服训练器中心化架构的局限性，使RL系统更灵活地支持复杂的智能体工作负载和多样化的计算资源配置，为LLM RL系统的可扩展性和经济性提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to improve the reasoning, coding, and tool-use capabilities of large language models, but agentic RL remains prohibitively expensive. Scaling RL to agentic LLMs requires supporting complex workloads, including multi-policy collaborative training, while efficiently using elastic, heterogeneous, and cross-region compute resources. Existing LLM RL systems support some of these capabilities, but each new extension often requires dedicated system engineering. This burden arises from trainer-centered control architectures and the lack of principled abstractions for RL system components. To address these limitations, we propose AstraFlow, a dataflow-oriented RL system that replaces conventional trainer-centered control with principled component abstractions. In AstraFlow, rollout services, dataflow management, and training are decoupled into autonomous components, enabling the system to natively support complex multi-policy agentic RL workloads and efficiently exploit diverse compute resources. We evaluate AstraFlow across math, code, search, and AgentBench workloads, showing that the same system supports multi-policy training, elastic scaling, heterogeneous cross-region execution, and composable data algorithms without system-level code changes. In multi-policy collaborative training, AstraFlow achieves comparable or better accuracy than existing RL systems while speeding up training time by 2.7x.

</details>


### [180] [Gaussian Relational Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15575)
*Zezhong Ding,Jin Li,Xugang Wang,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GelGT（高斯关系图Transformer），通过结构-语义协作采样保留结构连通性并过滤无关语义信息，结合高斯图注意力机制动态编码时序依赖，在多个真实数据集上取得了最高13.8%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系图学习方法在消息传递中因信息衰减难以捕获长程依赖，而当前的关系图Transformer未能联合建模结构、语义和时序信息。

Method: 提出GelGT模型，包含两个核心模块：(1) 结构-语义协作采样策略，在保留结构连通性的同时过滤无关语义信息；(2) 高斯图注意力机制，在采样子图上引入可学习的高斯偏置以动态编码时序依赖。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上，GelGT达到了最先进的下游任务性能，预测性能提升高达13.8%。

Conclusion: GelGT有效解决了关系图学习中长程依赖捕获不足以及结构、语义、时序信息联合建模困难的问题，验证了其在关系图学习任务上的优越性。

Abstract: Relational graph learning models relational databases as graphs and has demonstrated superior performance on a wide range of relational predictive tasks. However, existing methods struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to information decay in their message-passing mechanisms, and recent relational graph transformers remain limited in jointly modeling structural, semantic, and temporal information. In this paper, we propose GelGT, a Gaussian relational graph transformer that explicitly addresses these challenges. GelGT introduces a structure-semantic collaborative sampling strategy to preserve structural connectivity while filtering irrelevant semantic information, and incorporates a Gaussian graph attention mechanism with a learnable Gaussian bias on the sampled subgraphs to dynamically encode temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate that GelGT achieves state-of-the-art downstream task performance, with up to a 13.8% improvement in predictive performance.

</details>


### [181] [Embracing Biased Transition Matrices for Complementary-Label Learning with Many Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15586)
*Tan-Ha Mai,Chao-Kai Chiang,Han-Hwa Shih,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BICL框架，通过设计有偏（非均匀）的互补标签生成过程，将互补标签限制在类别子集内，解决了互补标签学习在大规模分类任务中的瓶颈，在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200上实现超过7倍的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统互补标签学习方法假设标签生成过程是均匀的，这在大规模分类任务中严重稀释了学习信号，导致方法难以扩展到多类别场景。本文旨在解决这一长期存在的瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出BICL（Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling）框架：通过设计有偏（非均匀）的互补标签生成过程，将互补标签限制在类别的子集内，从而增强学习信号。该框架覆盖从数据收集到训练的完整流程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200数据集上，BICL相比传统方法实现了超过7倍的准确率提升，显著突破了互补标签学习在大规模分类任务上的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文证明了有偏的互补标签生成策略可以有效克服大规模分类中的学习信号稀释问题，为互补标签学习在真实世界多类别应用中的可行性开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Complementary-label learning (CLL) is a weakly supervised paradigm where instances are labeled with classes they do not belong to. Despite a decade of research, CLL methods remain competitive mainly on 10-class classification, with scaling to large label spaces continuing to be an enduring bottleneck. This limitation stems from the common assumption of uniform label generation in traditional methods, which fatally dilutes the learning signal in many-class settings. In this paper, we demonstrate that this long-standing barrier can be overcome by deliberately designing a biased (non-uniform) generation process that restricts complementary labels to a subset of classes. This finding motivates us to propose Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL), a principled framework spanning data collection to training that leverages this bias. BICL enables effective learning on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet-200, achieving more than sevenfold accuracy improvements over traditional methods. Our findings establish a new trajectory for making CLL feasible for many classes in real-world applications.

</details>


### [182] [Offline Reinforcement Learning with Universal Horizon Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15603)
*Hojun Chung,Junseo Lee,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG
